# 網頁開發規格與功能需求
_Exported on 10/27/2025 at 20:54:32 GMT+8 from Cursor (1.7.46)_

---

**User**

以下是我這次網頁的規定開發規格：

### 🔹 前端

- **技術棧**：React + TypeScript（建議使用 Vite 建置）
- **主要套件**：React Router (前端 routing)、Axios (與後端的 HTTP 溝通)
- **UI 框架**：Material UI / Ant Design / Shadcn / TailwindCSS（擇一或混用）
- **Google Maps SDK**：使用 **Google Maps JavaScript API** 處理地圖顯示與互動
- **最低要求**
    - 地圖載入與基本操作（縮放、拖曳）
    - 可「搜尋」或「標記」地點（任一即可）
    - 使用者登入後才能針對 地點表單之類的資料 進行 新增/編輯/刪除（以頁面/按鈕狀態反映）

### 🔹 後端

- **技術棧**：Node.js + Express（建議 TypeScript）
- **RESTful API**：至少包含
    - `/auth`（註冊、登入、登出）
    - **一到兩個自定義資源**（例如 `/locations`、`/events`、`/posts`、`/items`…）具備 CRUD
- **Google Maps 伺服器端整合：**至少串接 **Geocoding** 或 **Places** 或 **Directions** 任一項（依主題選擇最合理者）
- **資料庫**：使用 SQLite（也可選 MongoDB 或 PostgreSQL）
    - 至少儲存「使用者登入資訊」或「主要資源資料」其中之一（建議兩者皆存）

**效能評估與優化**：建議寫一些 monitors 來評估前後端在處理 Google Maps requests 時的效能，必要時做一些優化。 

以下是我想做的主題：

我想要選擇以下主題： 
餐廳收藏及探索：
此網頁可以讓使用者選擇並收藏指定的一些餐廳，並有以下功能：
 - 收藏：從地圖上選取餐廳，將餐廳加到清單中，加上留言備註，讓使用者可以隨時瀏覽和更新
- 清單/標籤系統：地點可以自訂標籤（「午餐」「西式」「拉麵」），清單過濾。
- 收藏狀態：依照常去 / 去過 / 想去 等類別切換。
- 評分：每次拜訪餐廳時可以對它進行評分；地圖氣泡卡上就能打星；點進去才寫詳細拜訪紀錄。
 拜訪紀錄：每次拜訪餐廳時可以記錄時間、評分、備註、加入餐點、價錢等
- 拜訪統計：每家店平均分數、總花費、月度造訪次數（迷你 KPI）。
- 行事曆/時間軸檢視：按月檢視拜訪紀錄（可從日曆點一天帶出該日所有紀錄）。
- 地圖↔列表雙向互動：點列表 → 地圖定位；點地圖標記 → 右側彈出卡片。

請幫我依照以上的規則和想法，並以hw4資料夾作為 vite 專案的資料夾，先用假資料完成前端頁面，先確保前端可操作、頁面導覽正確

```typescript
// 使用者相關型別
export interface User {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  avatar?: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

// 餐廳相關型別
export interface Restaurant {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  phone?: string;
  website?: string;
  description?: string;
  images?: string[];
  tags: string[];
  status: RestaurantStatus;
  averageRating?: number;
  totalVisits: number;
  totalSpent: number;
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

// 餐廳狀態
export enum RestaurantStatus {
  WANT_TO_VISIT = 'want_to_visit',
  VISITED = 'visited',
  FREQUENT = 'frequent'
}

// 拜訪紀錄型別
export interface VisitRecord {
  id: string;
  restaurantId: string;
  userId: string;
  visitDate: Date;
  rating: number;
  comment?: string;
  dishes: Dish[];
  totalSpent: number;
  photos?: string[];
  createdAt: Date;
  updatedAt: Date;
}

// 餐點型別
export interface Dish {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  rating?: number;
  comment?: string;
}

// 標籤型別
export interface Tag {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  color: string;
  createdAt: Date;
}

// 地圖標記型別
export interface MapMarker {
  id: string;
  restaurant: Restaurant;
  position: {
    lat: number;
    lng: number;
  };
}

// 篩選選項型別
export interface FilterOptions {
  status?: RestaurantStatus[];
  tags?: string[];
  minRating?: number;
  maxRating?: number;
  searchQuery?: string;
}

// 統計資料型別
export interface RestaurantStats {
  totalRestaurants: number;
  totalVisits: number;
  totalSpent: number;
  averageRating: number;
  monthlyVisits: { month: string; count: number; spent: number }[];
  topRestaurants: Restaurant[];
}

// API 回應型別
export interface ApiResponse<T> {
  success: boolean;
  data?: T;
  error?: string;
  message?: string;
}

// 分頁型別
export interface PaginationParams {
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  sortBy?: string;
  sortOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc';
}

// 分頁回應型別
export interface PaginatedResponse<T> {
  data: T[];
  pagination: {
    page: number;
    limit: number;
    total: number;
    totalPages: number;
  };
}
```

現在創建 Context 來管理應用程式狀態：


```typescriptreact
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { User, Restaurant, VisitRecord, Tag, FilterOptions, RestaurantStatus } from '../types';
import { mockUser, mockRestaurants, mockVisitRecords, mockTags } from '../data/mockData';

interface AppContextType {
  user: User | null;
  restaurants: Restaurant[];
  visitRecords: VisitRecord[];
  tags: Tag[];
  filteredRestaurants: Restaurant[];
  filterOptions: FilterOptions;
  selectedRestaurant: Restaurant | null;
  setUser: (user: User | null) => void;
  setRestaurants: (restaurants: Restaurant[]) => void;
  setVisitRecords: (records: VisitRecord[]) => void;
  setTags: (tags: Tag[]) => void;
  setFilterOptions: (options: FilterOptions) => void;
  setSelectedRestaurant: (restaurant: Restaurant | null) => void;
  addRestaurant: (restaurant: Restaurant) => void;
  updateRestaurant: (id: string, updates: Partial<Restaurant>) => void;
  deleteRestaurant: (id: string) => void;
  addVisitRecord: (record: VisitRecord) => void;
  updateVisitRecord: (id: string, updates: Partial<VisitRecord>) => void;
  deleteVisitRecord: (id: string) => void;
  addTag: (tag: Tag) => void;
  filterRestaurants: () => void;
}

const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const useAppContext = () => {
  const context = useContext(AppContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAppContext must be used within an AppProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

interface AppProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

export const AppProvider: React.FC<AppProviderProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(mockUser);
  const [restaurants, setRestaurants] = useState<Restaurant[]>(mockRestaurants);
  const [visitRecords, setVisitRecords] = useState<VisitRecord[]>(mockVisitRecords);
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<Tag[]>(mockTags);
  const [filteredRestaurants, setFilteredRestaurants] = useState<Restaurant[]>(mockRestaurants);
  const [filterOptions, setFilterOptions] = useState<FilterOptions>({});
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState<Restaurant | null>(null);

  const filterRestaurants = () => {
    let filtered = [...restaurants];

    // 搜尋篩選
    if (filterOptions.searchQuery) {
      const query = filterOptions.searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        restaurant.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.address.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.tags.some(tag => tag.toLowerCase().includes(query))
      );
    }

    // 狀態篩選
    if (filterOptions.status && filterOptions.status.length > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        filterOptions.status!.includes(restaurant.status)
      );
    }

    // 標籤篩選
    if (filterOptions.tags && filterOptions.tags.length > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        filterOptions.tags!.some(tag => restaurant.tags.includes(tag))
      );
    }

    // 評分篩選
    if (filterOptions.minRating !== undefined) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        (restaurant.averageRating || 0) >= filterOptions.minRating!
      );
    }

    if (filterOptions.maxRating !== undefined) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        (restaurant.averageRating || 0) <= filterOptions.maxRating!
      );
    }

    setFilteredRestaurants(filtered);
  };

  const addRestaurant = (restaurant: Restaurant) => {
    setRestaurants(prev => [...prev, restaurant]);
  };

  const updateRestaurant = (id: string, updates: Partial<Restaurant>) => {
    setRestaurants(prev =>
      prev.map(restaurant =>
        restaurant.id === id ? { ...restaurant, ...updates } : restaurant
      )
    );
  };

  const deleteRestaurant = (id: string) => {
    setRestaurants(prev => prev.filter(restaurant => restaurant.id !== id));
    setVisitRecords(prev => prev.filter(record => record.restaurantId !== id));
  };

  const addVisitRecord = (record: VisitRecord) => {
    setVisitRecords(prev => [...prev, record]);
    
    // 更新餐廳統計資料
    const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === record.restaurantId);
    if (restaurant) {
      const newTotalVisits = restaurant.totalVisits + 1;
      const newTotalSpent = restaurant.totalSpent + record.totalSpent;
      const existingRecords = visitRecords.filter(r => r.restaurantId === record.restaurantId);
      const newAverageRating = existingRecords.length > 0 
        ? (existingRecords.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.rating, 0) + record.rating) / (existingRecords.length + 1)
        : record.rating;
      
      updateRestaurant(record.restaurantId, {
        totalVisits: newTotalVisits,
        totalSpent: newTotalSpent,
        averageRating: newAverageRating,
        status: newTotalVisits >= 5 ? RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT : RestaurantStatus.VISITED
      });
    }
  };

  const updateVisitRecord = (id: string, updates: Partial<VisitRecord>) => {
    setVisitRecords(prev =>
      prev.map(record =>
        record.id === id ? { ...record, ...updates } : record
      )
    );
  };

  const deleteVisitRecord = (id: string) => {
    const record = visitRecords.find(r => r.id === id);
    if (record) {
      setVisitRecords(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== id));
      
      // 更新餐廳統計資料
      const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === record.restaurantId);
      if (restaurant) {
        const newTotalVisits = Math.max(0, restaurant.totalVisits - 1);
        const newTotalSpent = Math.max(0, restaurant.totalSpent - record.totalSpent);
        const remainingRecords = visitRecords.filter(r => r.restaurantId === record.restaurantId && r.id !== id);
        const newAverageRating = remainingRecords.length > 0 
          ? remainingRecords.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.rating, 0) / remainingRecords.length
          : 0;
        
        updateRestaurant(record.restaurantId, {
          totalVisits: newTotalVisits,
          totalSpent: newTotalSpent,
          averageRating: newAverageRating,
          status: newTotalVisits >= 5 ? RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT : 
                 newTotalVisits > 0 ? RestaurantStatus.VISITED : RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT
        });
      }
    }
  };

  const addTag = (tag: Tag) => {
    setTags(prev => [...prev, tag]);
  };

  // 當篩選選項改變時自動重新篩選
  React.useEffect(() => {
    filterRestaurants();
  }, [filterOptions, restaurants]);

  const value: AppContextType = {
    user,
    restaurants,
    visitRecords,
    tags,
    filteredRestaurants,
    filterOptions,
    selectedRestaurant,
    setUser,
    setRestaurants,
    setVisitRecords,
    setTags,
    setFilterOptions,
    setSelectedRestaurant,
    addRestaurant,
    updateRestaurant,
    deleteRestaurant,
    addVisitRecord,
    updateVisitRecord,
    deleteVisitRecord,
    addTag,
    filterRestaurants,
  };

  return <AppContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AppContext.Provider>;
};
```

```typescriptreact
import { BrowserRouter as Router, Routes, Route } from 'react-router-dom';

// 創建 Material-UI 主題
const theme = createTheme({
  palette: {
    primary: {
      main: '#1976d2',
    },
    secondary: {
      main: '#dc004e',
    },
  },
  typography: {
    fontFamily: '"Roboto", "Helvetica", "Arial", sans-serif',
  },
});

function App() {
  return (
    <ThemeProvider theme={theme}>
      <CssBaseline />
      <AppProvider>
        <Router>
          <Layout>
            <Routes>
              <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
              <Route path="/restaurants" element={<RestaurantListPage />} />
              <Route path="/restaurants/:id" element={<RestaurantDetailPage />} />
              <Route path="/visits" element={<VisitRecordsPage />} />
              <Route path="/calendar" element={<CalendarPage />} />
              <Route path="/stats" element={<StatsPage />} />
            </Routes>
          </Layout>
        </Router>
      </AppProvider>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

export default App;
```

現在創建主要布局組件：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  AppBar,
  Toolbar,
  Typography,
  Button,
  IconButton,
  Drawer,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemIcon,
  ListItemText,
  Box,
  Avatar,
  Menu,
  MenuItem,
  useTheme,
  useMediaQuery,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Menu as MenuIcon,
  Restaurant,
  List as ListIcon,
  CalendarToday,
  BarChart,
  Home,
  AccountCircle,
  Logout,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

interface LayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const Layout: React.FC<LayoutProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [anchorEl, setAnchorEl] = useState<null | HTMLElement>(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { user } = useAppContext();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const isMobile = useMediaQuery(theme.breakpoints.down('md'));

  const menuItems = [
    { text: '首頁', icon: <Home />, path: '/' },
    { text: '餐廳列表', icon: <Restaurant />, path: '/restaurants' },
    { text: '拜訪紀錄', icon: <ListIcon />, path: '/visits' },
    { text: '行事曆', icon: <CalendarToday />, path: '/calendar' },
    { text: '統計資料', icon: <BarChart />, path: '/stats' },
  ];

  const handleDrawerToggle = () => {
    setDrawerOpen(!drawerOpen);
  };

  const handleMenuOpen = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
    setAnchorEl(event.currentTarget);
  };

  const handleMenuClose = () => {
    setAnchorEl(null);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    // 這裡可以添加登出邏輯
    handleMenuClose();
  };

  const handleNavigation = (path: string) => {
    navigate(path);
    if (isMobile) {
      setDrawerOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const drawer = (
    <Box sx={{ width: 250 }}>
      <Toolbar>
        <Typography variant="h6" noWrap component="div">
          美食探索
        </Typography>
      </Toolbar>
      <List>
        {menuItems.map((item) => (
          <ListItem
            button
            key={item.text}
            onClick={() => handleNavigation(item.path)}
            selected={location.pathname === item.path}
          >
            <ListItemIcon>{item.icon}</ListItemIcon>
            <ListItemText primary={item.text} />
          </ListItem>
        ))}
      </List>
    </Box>
  );

  return (
    <Box sx={{ display: 'flex' }}>
      <AppBar
        position="fixed"
        sx={{
          width: { md: `calc(100% - 250px)` },
          ml: { md: '250px' },
        }}
      >
        <Toolbar>
          <IconButton
            color="inherit"
            aria-label="open drawer"
            edge="start"
            onClick={handleDrawerToggle}
            sx={{ mr: 2, display: { md: 'none' } }}
          >
            <MenuIcon />
          </IconButton>
          <Typography variant="h6" noWrap component="div" sx={{ flexGrow: 1 }}>
            美食探索
          </Typography>
          {user && (
            <>
              <IconButton
                size="large"
                aria-label="account of current user"
                aria-controls="menu-appbar"
                aria-haspopup="true"
                onClick={handleMenuOpen}
                color="inherit"
              >
                <Avatar sx={{ width: 32, height: 32 }} src={user.avatar}>
                  {user.username.charAt(0)}
                </Avatar>
              </IconButton>
              <Menu
                id="menu-appbar"
                anchorEl={anchorEl}
                anchorOrigin={{
                  vertical: 'top',
                  horizontal: 'right',
                }}
                keepMounted
                transformOrigin={{
                  vertical: 'top',
                  horizontal: 'right',
                }}
                open={Boolean(anchorEl)}
                onClose={handleMenuClose}
              >
                <MenuItem onClick={handleMenuClose}>
                  <ListItemIcon>
                    <AccountCircle fontSize="small" />
                  </ListItemIcon>
                  個人資料
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuItem onClick={handleLogout}>
                  <ListItemIcon>
                    <Logout fontSize="small" />
                  </ListItemIcon>
                  登出
                </MenuItem>
              </Menu>
            </>
          )}
        </Toolbar>
      </AppBar>

      <Box
        component="nav"
        sx={{ width: { md: 250 }, flexShrink: { md: 0 } }}
      >
        <Drawer
          variant="temporary"
          open={drawerOpen}
          onClose={handleDrawerToggle}
          ModalProps={{
            keepMounted: true,
          }}
          sx={{
            display: { xs: 'block', md: 'none' },
            '& .MuiDrawer-paper': { boxSizing: 'border-box', width: 250 },
          }}
        >
          {drawer}
        </Drawer>
        <Drawer
          variant="permanent"
          sx={{
            display: { xs: 'none', md: 'block' },
            '& .MuiDrawer-paper': { boxSizing: 'border-box', width: 250 },
          }}
          open
        >
          {drawer}
        </Drawer>
      </Box>

      <Box
        component="main"
        sx={{
          flexGrow: 1,
          p: 3,
          width: { md: `calc(100% - 250px)` },
          mt: 8,
        }}
      >
        {children}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

export default Layout;
```

現在創建首頁：


```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Chip,
  Button,
  Paper,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Restaurant,
  Star,
  TrendingUp,
  CalendarToday,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';

const HomePage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { restaurants, visitRecords, user } = useAppContext();

  const stats = {
    totalRestaurants: restaurants.length,
    totalVisits: visitRecords.length,
    totalSpent: visitRecords.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.totalSpent, 0),
    averageRating: visitRecords.length > 0 
      ? visitRecords.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.rating, 0) / visitRecords.length 
      : 0,
  };

  const recentRestaurants = restaurants
    .filter(r => r.status === RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT)
    .slice(0, 3);

  const recentVisits = visitRecords
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime())
    .slice(0, 3);

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Typography variant="h3" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          歡迎回來，{user?.username}！
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
          繼續探索美食世界
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 統計卡片 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Restaurant color="primary" sx={{ mr: 2 }} />
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    收藏餐廳
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.totalRestaurants}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Star color="primary" sx={{ mr: 2 }} />
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.totalVisits}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <TrendingUp color="primary" sx={{ mr: 2 }} />
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {stats.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Star color="primary" sx={{ mr: 2 }} />
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {/* 常去餐廳 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={6}>
          <Paper sx={{ p: 3 }}>
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
              <Typography variant="h5" component="h2">
                常去餐廳
              </Typography>
              <Button 
                variant="outlined" 
                size="small"
                onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants')}
              >
                查看全部
              </Button>
            </Box>
            {recentRestaurants.length > 0 ? (
              recentRestaurants.map((restaurant) => (
                <Card key={restaurant.id} sx={{ mb: 2, cursor: 'pointer' }} 
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}`)}>
                  <CardContent>
                    <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                      {restaurant.name}
                    </Typography>
                    <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                      {restaurant.address}
                    </Typography>
                    <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1, mt: 1 }}>
                      <Chip 
                        label={restaurant.status === RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT ? '常去' : 
                               restaurant.status === RestaurantStatus.VISITED ? '去過' : '想去'} 
                        size="small" 
                        color="primary" 
                      />
                      {restaurant.averageRating && (
                        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                          <Star sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5 }} />
                          <Typography variant="body2">
                            {restaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                          </Typography>
                        </Box>
                      )}
                      <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                        拜訪 {restaurant.totalVisits} 次
                      </Typography>
                    </Box>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              ))
            ) : (
              <Typography color="text.secondary">
                還沒有常去的餐廳
              </Typography>
            )}
          </Paper>
        </Grid>

        {/* 最近拜訪 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={6}>
          <Paper sx={{ p: 3 }}>
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
              <Typography variant="h5" component="h2">
                最近拜訪
              </Typography>
              <Button 
                variant="outlined" 
                size="small"
                onClick={() => navigate('/visits')}
              >
                查看全部
              </Button>
            </Box>
            {recentVisits.length > 0 ? (
              recentVisits.map((visit) => {
                const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                return (
                  <Card key={visit.id} sx={{ mb: 2, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${visit.restaurantId}`)}>
                    <CardContent>
                      <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                        {restaurant?.name}
                      </Typography>
                      <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                        {new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')}
                      </Typography>
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1, mt: 1 }}>
                        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                          <Star sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5 }} />
                          <Typography variant="body2">
                            {visit.rating}
                          </Typography>
                        </Box>
                        <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                          NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                        </Typography>
                        {visit.comment && (
                          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                            "{visit.comment}"
                          </Typography>
                        )}
                      </Box>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                );
              })
            ) : (
              <Typography color="text.secondary">
                還沒有拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
            )}
          </Paper>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default HomePage;
```

現在創建餐廳列表頁面：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardMedia,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Chip,
  Button,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  OutlinedInput,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
  Rating,
  Fab,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  FilterList,
  Add,
  LocationOn,
  Star,
  Phone,
  Language,
  Edit,
  Delete,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';
import GoogleMap from '../components/GoogleMap';

const RestaurantListPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { 
    filteredRestaurants, 
    tags, 
    filterOptions, 
    setFilterOptions,
    deleteRestaurant 
  } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState(filterOptions.searchQuery || '');
  const [selectedStatus, setSelectedStatus] = useState<RestaurantStatus[]>(
    filterOptions.status || []
  );
  const [selectedTags, setSelectedTags] = useState<string[]>(
    filterOptions.tags || []
  );
  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false);
  const [showMap, setShowMap] = useState(false);
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [restaurantToDelete, setRestaurantToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSearch = (query: string) => {
    setSearchQuery(query);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, searchQuery: query });
  };

  const handleStatusChange = (status: RestaurantStatus[]) => {
    setSelectedStatus(status);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, status: status.length > 0 ? status : undefined });
  };

  const handleTagsChange = (tags: string[]) => {
    setSelectedTags(tags);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, tags: tags.length > 0 ? tags : undefined });
  };

  const handleDeleteClick = (restaurantId: string) => {
    setRestaurantToDelete(restaurantId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (restaurantToDelete) {
      deleteRestaurant(restaurantToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setRestaurantToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  const getStatusLabel = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return '想去';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return '去過';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return '常去';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return 'default';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return 'primary';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return 'secondary';
      default:
        return 'default';
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1">
          餐廳列表
        </Typography>
        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
          <Button
            variant={showMap ? "contained" : "outlined"}
            onClick={() => setShowMap(!showMap)}
            startIcon={<LocationOn />}
          >
            {showMap ? '列表檢視' : '地圖檢視'}
          </Button>
          <Fab
            color="primary"
            size="medium"
            onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants/new')}
          >
            <Add />
          </Fab>
        </Box>
      </Box>

      {/* 搜尋和篩選 */}
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Grid container spacing={2} alignItems="center">
          <Grid item xs={12} md={6}>
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              placeholder="搜尋餐廳名稱、地址或標籤..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => handleSearch(e.target.value)}
              InputProps={{
                startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
              }}
            />
          </Grid>
          <Grid item xs={12} md={6}>
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
              <Button
                variant="outlined"
                onClick={() => setShowFilters(!showFilters)}
                startIcon={<FilterList />}
              >
                篩選
              </Button>
              {(searchQuery || selectedStatus.length > 0 || selectedTags.length > 0) && (
                <Button
                  variant="text"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSearchQuery('');
                    setSelectedStatus([]);
                    setSelectedTags([]);
                    setFilterOptions({});
                  }}
                >
                  清除
                </Button>
              )}
            </Box>
          </Grid>
        </Grid>

        {showFilters && (
          <Box sx={{ mt: 2, p: 2, border: '1px solid', borderColor: 'divider', borderRadius: 1 }}>
            <Grid container spacing={2}>
              <Grid item xs={12} sm={6}>
                <FormControl fullWidth>
                  <InputLabel>狀態</InputLabel>
                  <Select
                    multiple
                    value={selectedStatus}
                    onChange={(e) => handleStatusChange(e.target.value as RestaurantStatus[])}
                    input={<OutlinedInput label="狀態" />}
                    renderValue={(selected) => (
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5 }}>
                        {selected.map((value) => (
                          <Chip key={value} label={getStatusLabel(value)} size="small" />
                        ))}
                      </Box>
                    )}
                  >
                    {Object.values(RestaurantStatus).map((status) => (
                      <MenuItem key={status} value={status}>
                        {getStatusLabel(status)}
                      </MenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </Select>
                </FormControl>
              </Grid>
              <Grid item xs={12} sm={6}>
                <FormControl fullWidth>
                  <InputLabel>標籤</InputLabel>
                  <Select
                    multiple
                    value={selectedTags}
                    onChange={(e) => handleTagsChange(e.target.value as string[])}
                    input={<OutlinedInput label="標籤" />}
                    renderValue={(selected) => (
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5 }}>
                        {selected.map((value) => (
                          <Chip key={value} label={value} size="small" />
                        ))}
                      </Box>
                    )}
                  >
                    {tags.map((tag) => (
                      <MenuItem key={tag.id} value={tag.name}>
                        {tag.name}
                      </MenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </Select>
                </FormControl>
              </Grid>
            </Grid>
          </Box>
        )}
      </Box>

      {/* 地圖檢視 */}
      {showMap && (
        <Box sx={{ mb: 3, height: 400 }}>
          <GoogleMap restaurants={filteredRestaurants} />
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 餐廳列表 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {filteredRestaurants.map((restaurant) => (
          <Grid item xs={12} sm={6} md={4} key={restaurant.id}>
            <Card sx={{ height: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
              {restaurant.images && restaurant.images.length > 0 && (
                <CardMedia
                  component="img"
                  height="200"
                  image={restaurant.images[0]}
                  alt={restaurant.name}
                />
              )}
              <CardContent sx={{ flexGrow: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                  {restaurant.name}
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  {restaurant.address}
                </Typography>
                
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5, mb: 2 }}>
                  <Chip 
                    label={getStatusLabel(restaurant.status)} 
                    size="small" 
                    color={getStatusColor(restaurant.status) as any}
                  />
                  {restaurant.tags.map((tag) => (
                    <Chip key={tag} label={tag} size="small" variant="outlined" />
                  ))}
                </Box>

                {restaurant.averageRating && (
                  <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 1 }}>
                    <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating} readOnly size="small" />
                    <Typography variant="body2" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                      {restaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)} ({restaurant.totalVisits} 次拜訪)
                    </Typography>
                  </Box>
                )}

                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總花費: NT$ {restaurant.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                </Typography>
              </CardContent>
              
              <Box sx={{ p: 2, pt: 0, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <Button
                  variant="contained"
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}`)}
                >
                  查看詳情
                </Button>
                <Box>
                  <IconButton
                    size="small"
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}/edit`)}
                  >
                    <Edit />
                  </IconButton>
                  <IconButton
                    size="small"
                    onClick={() => handleDeleteClick(restaurant.id)}
                    color="error"
                  >
                    <Delete />
                  </IconButton>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </Card>
          </Grid>
        ))}
      </Grid>

      {filteredRestaurants.length === 0 && (
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            沒有找到符合條件的餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
            試試調整搜尋條件或新增餐廳
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個餐廳嗎？此操作無法復原，相關的拜訪紀錄也會被刪除。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default RestaurantListPage;
```

現在創建 Google Maps 組件：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Box, Paper, Typography, Chip, Rating, Button } from '@mui/material';
import { LocationOn, Star, Phone, Language } from '@mui/icons-material';
import { Restaurant } from '../types';

interface GoogleMapProps {
  restaurants: Restaurant[];
  onRestaurantSelect?: (restaurant: Restaurant) => void;
  center?: { lat: number; lng: number };
  zoom?: number;
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    google: any;
    initMap: () => void;
  }
}

const GoogleMap: React.FC<GoogleMapProps> = ({ 
  restaurants, 
  onRestaurantSelect,
  center = { lat: 25.0330, lng: 121.5654 }, // 台北市預設位置
  zoom = 12 
}) => {
  const mapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const markersRef = useRef<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState<Restaurant | null>(null);
  const [mapLoaded, setMapLoaded] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 檢查 Google Maps API 是否已載入
    if (window.google && window.google.maps) {
      initializeMap();
    } else {
      // 如果沒有 API key，使用模擬地圖
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not found. Using mock map.');
      setMapLoaded(true);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mapInstanceRef.current && restaurants.length > 0) {
      updateMarkers();
    }
  }, [restaurants, mapLoaded]);

  const initializeMap = () => {
    if (!mapRef.current) return;

    mapInstanceRef.current = new window.google.maps.Map(mapRef.current, {
      center: center,
      zoom: zoom,
      mapTypeId: 'roadmap',
    });

    setMapLoaded(true);
  };

  const updateMarkers = () => {
    // 清除現有標記
    markersRef.current.forEach(marker => marker.setMap(null));
    markersRef.current = [];

    restaurants.forEach((restaurant) => {
      if (!mapInstanceRef.current) return;

      const marker = new window.google.maps.Marker({
        position: { lat: restaurant.latitude, lng: restaurant.longitude },
        map: mapInstanceRef.current,
        title: restaurant.name,
        icon: getMarkerIcon(restaurant),
      });

      // 添加點擊事件
      marker.addListener('click', () => {
        setSelectedRestaurant(restaurant);
        if (onRestaurantSelect) {
          onRestaurantSelect(restaurant);
        }
      });

      markersRef.current.push(marker);
    });
  };

  const getMarkerIcon = (restaurant: Restaurant) => {
    // 根據餐廳狀態返回不同的圖標顏色
    let color = '#FF5722'; // 預設紅色
    switch (restaurant.status) {
      case 'want_to_visit':
        color = '#9E9E9E'; // 灰色
        break;
      case 'visited':
        color = '#2196F3'; // 藍色
        break;
      case 'frequent':
        color = '#4CAF50'; // 綠色
        break;
    }

    return {
      url: `data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,${encodeURIComponent(`
        <svg width="32" height="32" viewBox="0 0 32 32" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <circle cx="16" cy="16" r="12" fill="${color}" stroke="white" stroke-width="2"/>
          <text x="16" y="20" text-anchor="middle" fill="white" font-size="16" font-family="Arial">餐</text>
        </svg>
      `)}`,
      scaledSize: new window.google.maps.Size(32, 32),
    };
  };

  const handleRestaurantCardClick = (restaurant: Restaurant) => {
    if (onRestaurantSelect) {
      onRestaurantSelect(restaurant);
    }
    setSelectedRestaurant(restaurant);
  };

  // 如果沒有載入 Google Maps，顯示模擬地圖
  if (!mapLoaded || !window.google) {
    return (
      <Paper sx={{ height: 400, p: 3, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 64, color: 'text.secondary', mb: 2 }} />
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            地圖載入中...
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
            請設定 Google Maps API Key 以啟用地圖功能
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      </Paper>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Box sx={{ position: 'relative', height: 400 }}>
      <div ref={mapRef} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', borderRadius: 8 }} />
      
      {/* 選中的餐廳資訊卡片 */}
      {selectedRestaurant && (
        <Paper
          sx={{
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 16,
            right: 16,
            width: 300,
            p: 2,
            maxHeight: 300,
            overflow: 'auto',
          }}
        >
          <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
            {selectedRestaurant.name}
          </Typography>
          
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
            <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
            {selectedRestaurant.address}
          </Typography>

          {selectedRestaurant.averageRating && (
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 1 }}>
              <Rating value={selectedRestaurant.averageRating} readOnly size="small" />
              <Typography variant="body2" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                {selectedRestaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)} ({selectedRestaurant.totalVisits} 次拜訪)
              </Typography>
            </Box>
          )}

          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5, mb: 2 }}>
            {selectedRestaurant.tags.map((tag) => (
              <Chip key={tag} label={tag} size="small" />
            ))}
          </Box>

          {selectedRestaurant.phone && (
            <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
              <Phone sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
              {selectedRestaurant.phone}
            </Typography>
          )}

          {selectedRestaurant.website && (
            <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
              <Language sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
              {selectedRestaurant.website}
            </Typography>
          )}

          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
            總花費: NT$ {selectedRestaurant.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
          </Typography>

          <Button
            variant="contained"
            size="small"
            fullWidth
            onClick={() => handleRestaurantCardClick(selectedRestaurant)}
            sx={{ mt: 1 }}
          >
            查看詳情
          </Button>
        </Paper>
      )}
    </Box>
  );
};

export default GoogleMap;
```

現在創建餐廳詳情頁面：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Container,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Chip,
  Button,
  Rating,
  Avatar,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  OutlinedInput,
  Divider,
  Paper,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemText,
  ListItemAvatar,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  ArrowBack,
  LocationOn,
  Phone,
  Language,
  Star,
  Edit,
  Add,
  Delete,
  CalendarToday,
  AttachMoney,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus, VisitRecord } from '../types';
import GoogleMap from '../components/GoogleMap';

const RestaurantDetailPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { 
    restaurants, 
    visitRecords, 
    tags, 
    addVisitRecord,
    updateRestaurant,
    deleteVisitRecord 
  } = useAppContext();

  const [showAddVisitDialog, setShowAddVisitDialog] = useState(false);
  const [showEditDialog, setShowEditDialog] = useState(false);
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visitToDelete, setVisitToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === id);
  
  if (!restaurant) {
    return (
      <Container maxWidth="lg">
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            找不到指定的餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Button
            variant="contained"
            onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants')}
            sx={{ mt: 2 }}
          >
            返回餐廳列表
          </Button>
        </Box>
      </Container>
    );
  }

  const restaurantVisits = visitRecords
    .filter(record => record.restaurantId === restaurant.id)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime());

  const getStatusLabel = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return '想去';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return '去過';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return '常去';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return 'default';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return 'primary';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return 'secondary';
      default:
        return 'default';
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteVisit = (visitId: string) => {
    setVisitToDelete(visitId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (visitToDelete) {
      deleteVisitRecord(visitToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setVisitToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      {/* 返回按鈕 */}
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Button
          startIcon={<ArrowBack />}
          onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants')}
        >
          返回餐廳列表
        </Button>
      </Box>

      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {/* 餐廳基本資訊 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={8}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', mb: 2 }}>
                <Typography variant="h4" component="h1">
                  {restaurant.name}
                </Typography>
                <Box>
                  <Chip 
                    label={getStatusLabel(restaurant.status)} 
                    color={getStatusColor(restaurant.status) as any}
                    sx={{ mr: 1 }}
                  />
                  <IconButton onClick={() => setShowEditDialog(true)}>
                    <Edit />
                  </IconButton>
                </Box>
              </Box>

              <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                {restaurant.address}
              </Typography>

              {restaurant.phone && (
                <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <Phone sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  {restaurant.phone}
                </Typography>
              )}

              {restaurant.website && (
                <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <Language sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  <a href={restaurant.website} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    {restaurant.website}
                  </a>
                </Typography>
              )}

              {restaurant.description && (
                <Typography variant="body1" sx={{ mt: 2 }}>
                  {restaurant.description}
                </Typography>
              )}

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 1, mt: 2 }}>
                {restaurant.tags.map((tag) => (
                  <Chip key={tag} label={tag} variant="outlined" />
                ))}
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* 地圖 */}
          <Card sx={{ mt: 3 }}>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                位置
              </Typography>
              <GoogleMap 
                restaurants={[restaurant]} 
                center={{ lat: restaurant.latitude, lng: restaurant.longitude }}
                zoom={15}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 統計資訊和拜訪紀錄 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={4}>
          {/* 統計資訊 */}
          <Card sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                統計資訊
              </Typography>
              
              {restaurant.averageRating && (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
                  <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating} readOnly />
                  <Typography variant="h6" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                    {restaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              )}

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總拜訪次數:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  {restaurant.totalVisits}
                </Typography>
              </Box>

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總花費:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  NT$ {restaurant.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                </Typography>
              </Box>

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  平均花費:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  NT$ {restaurant.totalVisits > 0 ? Math.round(restaurant.totalSpent / restaurant.totalVisits).toLocaleString() : 0}
                </Typography>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* 拜訪紀錄 */}
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
                <Typography variant="h6">
                  拜訪紀錄
                </Typography>
                <Button
                  variant="contained"
                  size="small"
                  startIcon={<Add />}
                  onClick={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(true)}
                >
                  新增拜訪
                </Button>
              </Box>

              {restaurantVisits.length > 0 ? (
                <List>
                  {restaurantVisits.map((visit) => (
                    <ListItem key={visit.id} divider>
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <CalendarToday />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="body2">
                              {new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')}
                            </Typography>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                          </Box>
                        }
                        secondary={
                          <Box>
                            <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                              <AttachMoney sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                              NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                            </Typography>
                            {visit.comment && (
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                {visit.comment}
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                          </Box>
                        }
                      />
                      <IconButton
                        size="small"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteVisit(visit.id)}
                        color="error"
                      >
                        <Delete />
                      </IconButton>
                    </ListItem>
                  ))}
                </List>
              ) : (
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 2 }}>
                  還沒有拜訪紀錄
                </Typography>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 新增拜訪對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={showAddVisitDialog} onClose={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)} maxWidth="sm" fullWidth>
        <DialogTitle>新增拜訪紀錄</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
            為 {restaurant.name} 新增拜訪紀錄
          </Typography>
          <AddVisitForm 
            restaurant={restaurant} 
            onClose={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)}
            onSave={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個拜訪紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

// 新增拜訪表單組件
const AddVisitForm: React.FC<{
  restaurant: any;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}> = ({ restaurant, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const { addVisitRecord } = useAppContext();
  const [visitDate, setVisitDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<number>(5);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('');
  const [totalSpent, setTotalSpent] = useState(0);

  const handleSubmit = () => {
    const newVisit = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      restaurantId: restaurant.id,
      userId: '1',
      visitDate: new Date(visitDate),
      rating,
      comment,
      dishes: [],
      totalSpent,
      photos: [],
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    };

    addVisitRecord(newVisit);
    onSave();
  };

  return (
    <Box sx={{ pt: 2 }}>
      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="拜訪日期"
        type="date"
        value={visitDate}
        onChange={(e) => setVisitDate(e.target.value)}
        InputLabelProps={{ shrink: true }}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />
      
      <Box sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
        <Typography variant="body2" gutterBottom>
          評分
        </Typography>
        <Rating
          value={rating}
          onChange={(event, newValue) => setRating(newValue || 5)}
        />
      </Box>

      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="總花費 (NT$)"
        type="number"
        value={totalSpent}
        onChange={(e) => setTotalSpent(Number(e.target.value))}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />

      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="備註"
        multiline
        rows={3}
        value={comment}
        onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />

      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 1 }}>
        <Button onClick={onClose}>取消</Button>
        <Button variant="contained" onClick={handleSubmit}>
          儲存
        </Button>
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

export default RestaurantDetailPage;
```

現在創建拜訪紀錄頁面：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Grid,
  Avatar,
  Rating,
  Chip,
  Button,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemAvatar,
  ListItemText,
  Divider,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  CalendarToday,
  AttachMoney,
  Star,
  Restaurant,
  FilterList,
  Delete,
  Edit,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

const VisitRecordsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { visitRecords, restaurants, deleteVisitRecord } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState('');
  const [minRating, setMinRating] = useState('');
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visitToDelete, setVisitToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // 篩選拜訪紀錄
  const filteredVisits = visitRecords.filter(visit => {
    const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
    if (!restaurant) return false;

    // 搜尋篩選
    if (searchQuery && !restaurant.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) &&
        !visit.comment?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) {
      return false;
    }

    // 餐廳篩選
    if (selectedRestaurant && visit.restaurantId !== selectedRestaurant) {
      return false;
    }

    // 評分篩選
    if (minRating && visit.rating < Number(minRating)) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime());

  const handleDeleteClick = (visitId: string) => {
    setVisitToDelete(visitId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (visitToDelete) {
      deleteVisitRecord(visitToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setVisitToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchQuery('');
    setSelectedRestaurant('');
    setMinRating('');
  };

  const totalSpent = filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.totalSpent, 0);
  const averageRating = filteredVisits.length > 0 
    ? filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.rating, 0) / filteredVisits.length 
    : 0;

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          查看和管理你的餐廳拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 統計資訊 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <CalendarToday />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {filteredVisits.length}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'success.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <AttachMoney />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Star />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 搜尋和篩選 */}
      <Card sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <CardContent>
          <Grid container spacing={2} alignItems="center">
            <Grid item xs={12} md={4}>
              <TextField
                fullWidth
                placeholder="搜尋餐廳或備註..."
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                InputProps={{
                  startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
                }}
              />
            </Grid>
            <Grid item xs={12} md={3}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>餐廳</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={selectedRestaurant}
                  onChange={(e) => setSelectedRestaurant(e.target.value)}
                  label="餐廳"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">全部餐廳</MenuItem>
                  {restaurants.map((restaurant) => (
                    <MenuItem key={restaurant.id} value={restaurant.id}>
                      {restaurant.name}
                    </MenuItem>
                  ))}
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid item xs={12} md={2}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>最低評分</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={minRating}
                  onChange={(e) => setMinRating(e.target.value)}
                  label="最低評分"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">不限</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="1">1 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="2">2 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="3">3 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="4">4 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="5">5 星</MenuItem>
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid item xs={12} md={3}>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
                <Button
                  variant="outlined"
                  startIcon={<FilterList />}
                  onClick={() => {/* 可以添加更多篩選選項 */}}
                >
                  更多篩選
                </Button>
                {(searchQuery || selectedRestaurant || minRating) && (
                  <Button
                    variant="text"
                    onClick={clearFilters}
                  >
                    清除
                  </Button>
                )}
              </Box>
            </Grid>
          </Grid>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 拜訪紀錄列表 */}
      <Card>
        <CardContent>
          <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
            拜訪紀錄 ({filteredVisits.length} 筆)
          </Typography>
          
          {filteredVisits.length > 0 ? (
            <List>
              {filteredVisits.map((visit, index) => {
                const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={visit.id}>
                    <ListItem
                      sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${visit.restaurantId}`)}
                    >
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <Restaurant />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1, mb: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="h6">
                              {restaurant?.name}
                            </Typography>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                            <Chip 
                              label={new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')} 
                              size="small" 
                              variant="outlined"
                            />
                          </Box>
                        }
                        secondary={
                          <Box>
                            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2, mb: 1 }}>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <AttachMoney sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                              </Typography>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                {restaurant?.address}
                              </Typography>
                            </Box>
                            {visit.comment && (
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                "{visit.comment}"
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                            {visit.dishes && visit.dishes.length > 0 && (
                              <Box sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                                {visit.dishes.map((dish) => (
                                  <Chip
                                    key={dish.id}
                                    label={`${dish.name} (NT$ ${dish.price})`}
                                    size="small"
                                    variant="outlined"
                                    sx={{ mr: 0.5, mb: 0.5 }}
                                  />
                                ))}
                              </Box>
                            )}
                          </Box>
                        }
                      />
                      <Box>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            // 可以添加編輯功能
                          }}
                        >
                          <Edit />
                        </IconButton>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleDeleteClick(visit.id);
                          }}
                          color="error"
                        >
                          <Delete />
                        </IconButton>
                      </Box>
                    </ListItem>
                    {index < filteredVisits.length - 1 && <Divider />}
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </List>
          ) : (
            <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
              <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
                沒有找到符合條件的拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                試試調整篩選條件或新增拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
            </Box>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個拜訪紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default VisitRecordsPage;
```

現在創建行事曆頁面：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Button,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemAvatar,
  ListItemText,
  Avatar,
  Rating,
  Chip,
  Divider,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  CalendarToday,
  Restaurant,
  AttachMoney,
  Star,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

const CalendarPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { visitRecords, restaurants } = useAppContext();
  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(new Date());
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [showDateDialog, setShowDateDialog] = useState(false);

  // 獲取當前月份的拜訪紀錄
  const getVisitsForMonth = (date: Date) => {
    return visitRecords.filter(visit => {
      const visitDate = new Date(visit.visitDate);
      return visitDate.getMonth() === date.getMonth() && 
             visitDate.getFullYear() === date.getFullYear();
    });
  };

  // 獲取指定日期的拜訪紀錄
  const getVisitsForDate = (date: Date) => {
    return visitRecords.filter(visit => {
      const visitDate = new Date(visit.visitDate);
      return visitDate.toDateString() === date.toDateString();
    });
  };

  // 生成日曆網格
  const generateCalendarGrid = () => {
    const year = currentDate.getFullYear();
    const month = currentDate.getMonth();
    const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
    const lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0);
    const startDate = new Date(firstDay);
    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - firstDay.getDay());
    
    const days = [];
    const today = new Date();
    
    for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      const date = new Date(startDate);
      date.setDate(startDate.getDate() + i);
      const visits = getVisitsForDate(date);
      
      days.push({
        date,
        visits,
        isCurrentMonth: date.getMonth() === month,
        isToday: date.toDateString() === today.toDateString(),
      });
    }
    
    return days;
  };

  const handlePrevMonth = () => {
    setCurrentDate(new Date(currentDate.getFullYear(), currentDate.getMonth() - 1, 1));
  };

  const handleNextMonth = () => {
    setCurrentDate(new Date(currentDate.getFullYear(), currentDate.getMonth() + 1, 1));
  };

  const handleDateClick = (date: Date) => {
    const visits = getVisitsForDate(date);
    if (visits.length > 0) {
      setSelectedDate(date);
      setShowDateDialog(true);
    }
  };

  const monthNames = [
    '一月', '二月', '三月', '四月', '五月', '六月',
    '七月', '八月', '九月', '十月', '十一月', '十二月'
  ];

  const weekDays = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];

  const calendarDays = generateCalendarGrid();
  const monthVisits = getVisitsForMonth(currentDate);

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          拜訪行事曆
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          查看你的餐廳拜訪時間軸
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 月度統計 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <CalendarToday />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    本月拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {monthVisits.length}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'success.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <AttachMoney />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    本月總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {monthVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.totalSpent, 0).toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid item xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Star />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    本月平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {monthVisits.length > 0 
                      ? (monthVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.rating, 0) / monthVisits.length).toFixed(1)
                      : '0.0'
                    }
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 日曆 */}
      <Card>
        <CardContent>
          {/* 月份導航 */}
          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 3 }}>
            <IconButton onClick={handlePrevMonth}>
              <ChevronLeft />
            </IconButton>
            <Typography variant="h5">
              {currentDate.getFullYear()}年 {monthNames[currentDate.getMonth()]}
            </Typography>
            <IconButton onClick={handleNextMonth}>
              <ChevronRight />
            </IconButton>
          </Box>

          {/* 星期標題 */}
          <Grid container spacing={1} sx={{ mb: 1 }}>
            {weekDays.map((day) => (
              <Grid item xs key={day}>
                <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 1 }}>
                  <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                    {day}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Grid>
            ))}
          </Grid>

          {/* 日期網格 */}
          <Grid container spacing={1}>
            {calendarDays.map((day, index) => (
              <Grid item xs key={index}>
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    minHeight: 100,
                    border: '1px solid',
                    borderColor: 'divider',
                    borderRadius: 1,
                    p: 1,
                    cursor: day.visits.length > 0 ? 'pointer' : 'default',
                    backgroundColor: day.isToday ? 'primary.light' : 'transparent',
                    opacity: day.isCurrentMonth ? 1 : 0.5,
                    '&:hover': day.visits.length > 0 ? {
                      backgroundColor: 'action.hover',
                    } : {},
                  }}
                  onClick={() => handleDateClick(day.date)}
                >
                  <Typography 
                    variant="body2" 
                    sx={{ 
                      fontWeight: day.isToday ? 'bold' : 'normal',
                      color: day.isToday ? 'primary.contrastText' : 'text.primary'
                    }}
                  >
                    {day.date.getDate()}
                  </Typography>
                  
                  {day.visits.length > 0 && (
                    <Box sx={{ mt: 0.5 }}>
                      {day.visits.slice(0, 2).map((visit) => {
                        const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                        return (
                          <Box key={visit.id} sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 0.5 }}>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                            <Typography variant="caption" sx={{ ml: 0.5, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
                              {restaurant?.name}
                            </Typography>
                          </Box>
                        );
                      })}
                      {day.visits.length > 2 && (
                        <Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">
                          +{day.visits.length - 2} 更多
                        </Typography>
                      )}
                    </Box>
                  )}
                </Box>
              </Grid>
            ))}
          </Grid>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 日期詳情對話框 */}
      <Dialog 
        open={showDateDialog} 
        onClose={() => setShowDateDialog(false)}
        maxWidth="sm"
        fullWidth
      >
        <DialogTitle>
          {selectedDate && selectedDate.toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')} 的拜訪紀錄
        </DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          {selectedDate && (
            <List>
              {getVisitsForDate(selectedDate).map((visit, index) => {
                const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={visit.id}>
                    <ListItem
                      sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                      onClick={() => {
                        navigate(`/restaurants/${visit.restaurantId}`);
                        setShowDateDialog(false);
                      }}
                    >
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <Restaurant />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="h6">
                              {restaurant?.name}
```
