```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardMedia,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Chip,
  Button,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  OutlinedInput,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
  Rating,
  Fab,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  FilterList,
  Add,
  LocationOn,
  Edit,
  Delete,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';
import GoogleMap from '../components/GoogleMap';

const RestaurantListPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { 
    filteredRestaurants, 
    tags, 
    filterOptions, 
    setFilterOptions,
    deleteRestaurant 
  } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState(filterOptions.searchQuery || '');
  const [selectedStatus, setSelectedStatus] = useState<RestaurantStatus[]>(
    filterOptions.status || []
  );
  const [selectedTags, setSelectedTags] = useState<string[]>(
    filterOptions.tags || []
  );
  const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false);
  const [showMap, setShowMap] = useState(false);
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [restaurantToDelete, setRestaurantToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSearch = (query: string) => {
    setSearchQuery(query);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, searchQuery: query });
  };

  const handleStatusChange = (status: RestaurantStatus[]) => {
    setSelectedStatus(status);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, status: status.length > 0 ? status : undefined });
  };

  const handleTagsChange = (tags: string[]) => {
    setSelectedTags(tags);
    setFilterOptions({ ...filterOptions, tags: tags.length > 0 ? tags : undefined });
  };

  const handleDeleteClick = (restaurantId: string) => {
    setRestaurantToDelete(restaurantId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (restaurantToDelete) {
      deleteRestaurant(restaurantToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setRestaurantToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  const getStatusLabel = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return '想去';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return '去過';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return '常去';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return 'default';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return 'primary';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return 'secondary';
      default:
        return 'default';
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1">
          餐廳列表
        </Typography>
        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
          <Button
            variant={showMap ? "contained" : "outlined"}
            onClick={() => setShowMap(!showMap)}
            startIcon={<LocationOn />}
          >
            {showMap ? '列表檢視' : '地圖檢視'}
          </Button>
          <Fab
            color="primary"
            size="medium"
            onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants/new')}
          >
            <Add />
          </Fab>
        </Box>
      </Box>

      {/* 搜尋和篩選 */}
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Grid container spacing={2} alignItems="center">
          <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              placeholder="搜尋餐廳名稱、地址或標籤..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => handleSearch(e.target.value)}
              InputProps={{
                startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
              }}
            />
          </Grid>
          <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
              <Button
                variant="outlined"
                onClick={() => setShowFilters(!showFilters)}
                startIcon={<FilterList />}
              >
                篩選
              </Button>
              {(searchQuery || selectedStatus.length > 0 || selectedTags.length > 0) && (
                <Button
                  variant="text"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSearchQuery('');
                    setSelectedStatus([]);
                    setSelectedTags([]);
                    setFilterOptions({});
                  }}
                >
                  清除
                </Button>
              )}
            </Box>
          </Grid>
        </Grid>

        {showFilters && (
          <Box sx={{ mt: 2, p: 2, border: '1px solid', borderColor: 'divider', borderRadius: 1 }}>
            <Grid container spacing={2}>
              <Grid xs={12} sm={6}>
                <FormControl fullWidth>
                  <InputLabel>狀態</InputLabel>
                  <Select
                    multiple
                    value={selectedStatus}
                    onChange={(e) => handleStatusChange(e.target.value as RestaurantStatus[])}
                    input={<OutlinedInput label="狀態" />}
                    renderValue={(selected) => (
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5 }}>
                        {selected.map((value) => (
                          <Chip key={value} label={getStatusLabel(value)} size="small" />
                        ))}
                      </Box>
                    )}
                  >
                    {Object.values(RestaurantStatus).map((status) => (
                      <MenuItem key={status} value={status}>
                        {getStatusLabel(status)}
                      </MenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </Select>
                </FormControl>
              </Grid>
              <Grid xs={12} sm={6}>
                <FormControl fullWidth>
                  <InputLabel>標籤</InputLabel>
                  <Select
                    multiple
                    value={selectedTags}
                    onChange={(e) => handleTagsChange(e.target.value as string[])}
                    input={<OutlinedInput label="標籤" />}
                    renderValue={(selected) => (
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5 }}>
                        {selected.map((value) => (
                          <Chip key={value} label={value} size="small" />
                        ))}
                      </Box>
                    )}
                  >
                    {tags.map((tag) => (
                      <MenuItem key={tag.id} value={tag.name}>
                        {tag.name}
                      </MenuItem>
                    ))}
                  </Select>
                </FormControl>
              </Grid>
            </Grid>
          </Box>
        )}
      </Box>

      {/* 地圖檢視 */}
      {showMap && (
        <Box sx={{ mb: 3, height: 400 }}>
          <GoogleMap restaurants={filteredRestaurants} />
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 餐廳列表 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {filteredRestaurants.map((restaurant) => (
          <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={4} key={restaurant.id}>
            <Card sx={{ height: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
              {restaurant.images && restaurant.images.length > 0 && (
                <CardMedia
                  component="img"
                  height="200"
                  image={restaurant.images[0]}
                  alt={restaurant.name}
                />
              )}
              <CardContent sx={{ flexGrow: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                  {restaurant.name}
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 16, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  {restaurant.address}
                </Typography>
                
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 0.5, mb: 2 }}>
                  <Chip 
                    label={getStatusLabel(restaurant.status)} 
                    size="small" 
                    color={getStatusColor(restaurant.status) as any}
                  />
                  {restaurant.tags.map((tag) => (
                    <Chip key={tag} label={tag} size="small" variant="outlined" />
                  ))}
                </Box>

                {restaurant.averageRating && (
                  <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 1 }}>
                    <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating} readOnly size="small" />
                    <Typography variant="body2" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                      {restaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)} ({restaurant.totalVisits} 次拜訪)
                    </Typography>
                  </Box>
                )}

                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總花費: NT$ {restaurant.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                </Typography>
              </CardContent>
              
              <Box sx={{ p: 2, pt: 0, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                <Button
                  variant="contained"
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}`)}
                >
                  查看詳情
                </Button>
                <Box>
                  <IconButton
                    size="small"
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}/edit`)}
                  >
                    <Edit />
                  </IconButton>
                  <IconButton
                    size="small"
                    onClick={() => handleDeleteClick(restaurant.id)}
                    color="error"
                  >
                    <Delete />
                  </IconButton>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </Card>
          </Grid>
        ))}
      </Grid>

      {filteredRestaurants.length === 0 && (
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            沒有找到符合條件的餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
            試試調整搜尋條件或新增餐廳
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個餐廳嗎？此操作無法復原，相關的拜訪紀錄也會被刪除。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default RestaurantListPage;

```

現在修復其他頁面的錯誤：


```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Paper,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemAvatar,
  ListItemText,
  Avatar,
  Rating,
  Chip,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Restaurant,
  Star,
  AttachMoney,
  CalendarToday,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';

const StatsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { restaurants, visitRecords, tags } = useAppContext();

  // 計算統計資料
  const stats = {
    totalRestaurants: restaurants.length,
    totalVisits: visitRecords.length,
    totalSpent: visitRecords.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.totalSpent, 0),
    averageRating: visitRecords.length > 0 
      ? visitRecords.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.rating, 0) / visitRecords.length 
      : 0,
    averageSpentPerVisit: visitRecords.length > 0 
      ? visitRecords.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.totalSpent, 0) / visitRecords.length 
      : 0,
  };

  // 按狀態分組餐廳
  const restaurantsByStatus = {
    [RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT]: restaurants.filter(r => r.status === RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT),
    [RestaurantStatus.VISITED]: restaurants.filter(r => r.status === RestaurantStatus.VISITED),
    [RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT]: restaurants.filter(r => r.status === RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT),
  };

  // 最受歡迎的餐廳（按拜訪次數）
  const topRestaurantsByVisits = [...restaurants]
    .filter(r => r.totalVisits > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.totalVisits - a.totalVisits)
    .slice(0, 5);

  // 最貴的餐廳（按總花費）
  // const topRestaurantsBySpending = [...restaurants]
  //   .filter(r => r.totalSpent > 0)
  //   .sort((a, b) => b.totalSpent - a.totalSpent)
  //   .slice(0, 5);

  // 評分最高的餐廳
  const topRestaurantsByRating = [...restaurants]
    .filter(r => r.averageRating && r.averageRating > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.averageRating || 0) - (a.averageRating || 0))
    .slice(0, 5);

  // 最受歡迎的標籤
  const tagStats = tags.map(tag => {
    const count = restaurants.filter(r => r.tags.includes(tag.name)).length;
    return { tag, count };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count).slice(0, 5);

  // 月度拜訪統計
  const monthlyStats = visitRecords.reduce((acc, visit) => {
    const month = new Date(visit.visitDate).toISOString().substring(0, 7); // YYYY-MM
    if (!acc[month]) {
      acc[month] = { count: 0, spent: 0 };
    }
    acc[month].count += 1;
    acc[month].spent += visit.totalSpent;
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, { count: number; spent: number }>);

  const monthlyData = Object.entries(monthlyStats)
    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .slice(-6); // 最近6個月

  const getStatusLabel = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return '想去';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return '去過';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return '常去';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return 'default';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return 'primary';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return 'secondary';
      default:
        return 'default';
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          統計資料
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          你的美食探索數據分析
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 通常是計統計 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Restaurant />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    收藏餐廳
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.totalRestaurants}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'success.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <CalendarToday />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.totalVisits}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <AttachMoney />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {stats.totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={3}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'info.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Star />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {stats.averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {/* 餐廳狀態分佈 */}
        <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                餐廳狀態分佈
              </Typography>
              {Object.entries(restaurantsByStatus).map(([status, restaurants]) => (
                <Box key={status} sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                  <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 1 }}>
                    <Chip 
                      label={getStatusLabel(status as RestaurantStatus)} 
                      color={getStatusColor(status as RestaurantStatus) as any}
                      size="small"
                    />
                    <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                      {restaurants.length} 家
                    </Typography>
                  </Box>
                  <Box sx={{ 
                    height: 8, 
                    backgroundColor: 'grey.200', 
                    borderRadius: 1,
                    overflow: 'hidden'
                  }}>
                    <Box sx={{ 
                      height: '100%', 
                      backgroundColor: status === RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT ? 'grey.500' :
                                      status === RestaurantStatus.VISITED ? 'primary.main' : 'secondary.main',
                      width: `${(restaurants.length / stats.totalRestaurants) * 100}%`,
                      transition: 'width 0.3s ease'
                    }} />
                  </Box>
                </Box>
              ))}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 最受歡迎標籤 */}
        <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                最受歡迎標籤
              </Typography>
              <List>
                {tagStats.map(({ tag, count }) => (
                  <ListItem key={tag.id}>
                    <ListItemAvatar>
                      <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: tag.color, width: 32, height: 32 }}>
                        {tag.name.charAt(0)}
                      </Avatar>
                    </ListItemAvatar>
                    <ListItemText
                      primary={tag.name}
                      secondary={`${count} 家餐廳`}
                    />
                  </ListItem>
                ))}
              </List>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 最受歡迎餐廳 */}
        <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                最常拜訪餐廳
              </Typography>
              <List>
                {topRestaurantsByVisits.map((restaurant, index) => (
                  <ListItem 
                    key={restaurant.id}
                    sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}`)}
                  >
                    <ListItemAvatar>
                      <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                        {index + 1}
                      </Avatar>
                    </ListItemAvatar>
                    <ListItemText
                      primary={restaurant.name}
                      secondary={
                        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                          <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating || 0} readOnly size="small" />
                          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                            {restaurant.totalVisits} 次拜訪
                          </Typography>
                        </Box>
                      }
                    />
                  </ListItem>
                ))}
              </List>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 評分最高餐廳 */}
        <Grid xs={12} md={6}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                評分最高餐廳
              </Typography>
              <List>
                {topRestaurantsByRating.map((restaurant, index) => (
                  <ListItem 
                    key={restaurant.id}
                    sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${restaurant.id}`)}
                  >
                    <ListItemAvatar>
                      <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main' }}>
                        {index + 1}
                      </Avatar>
                    </ListItemAvatar>
                    <ListItemText
                      primary={restaurant.name}
                      secondary={
                        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                          <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating || 0} readOnly size="small" />
                          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                            {restaurant.averageRating?.toFixed(1)} 分
                          </Typography>
                        </Box>
                      }
                    />
                  </ListItem>
                ))}
              </List>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 月度拜訪趨勢 */}
        <Grid xs={12}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                最近6個月拜訪趨勢
              </Typography>
              <Grid container spacing={2}>
                {monthlyData.map(([month, data]) => (
                  <Grid xs={12} sm={6} md={2} key={month}>
                    <Paper sx={{ p: 2, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                        {month}
                      </Typography>
                      <Typography variant="h6">
                        {data.count} 次
                      </Typography>
                      <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                        NT$ {data.spent.toLocaleString()}
                      </Typography>
                    </Paper>
                  </Grid>
                ))}
              </Grid>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default StatsPage;

```

讓我使用 replace_all 來修復 Grid 組件的問題：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Grid,
  Avatar,
  Rating,
  Chip,
  Button,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemAvatar,
  ListItemText,
  Divider,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  CalendarToday,
  AttachMoney,
  Star,
  Restaurant,
  FilterList,
  Delete,
  Edit,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

const VisitRecordsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { visitRecords, restaurants, deleteVisitRecord } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState('');
  const [minRating, setMinRating] = useState('');
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visitToDelete, setVisitToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // 篩選拜訪紀錄
  const filteredVisits = visitRecords.filter(visit => {
    const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
    if (!restaurant) return false;

    // 搜尋篩選
    if (searchQuery && !restaurant.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) &&
        !visit.comment?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) {
      return false;
    }

    // 餐廳篩選
    if (selectedRestaurant && visit.restaurantId !== selectedRestaurant) {
      return false;
    }

    // 評分篩選
    if (minRating && visit.rating < Number(minRating)) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime());

  const handleDeleteClick = (visitId: string) => {
    setVisitToDelete(visitId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (visitToDelete) {
      deleteVisitRecord(visitToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setVisitToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchQuery('');
    setSelectedRestaurant('');
    setMinRating('');
  };

  const totalSpent = filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.totalSpent, 0);
  const averageRating = filteredVisits.length > 0 
    ? filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.rating, 0) / filteredVisits.length 
    : 0;

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          查看和管理你的餐廳拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 統計資訊 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <CalendarToday />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {filteredVisits.length}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'success.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <AttachMoney />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Star />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 搜尋和篩選 */}
      <Card sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <CardContent>
          <Grid container spacing={2} alignItems="center">
            <Grid xs={12} md={4}>
              <TextField
                fullWidth
                placeholder="搜尋餐廳或備註..."
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                InputProps={{
                  startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
                }}
              />
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={3}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>餐廳</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={selectedRestaurant}
                  onChange={(e) => setSelectedRestaurant(e.target.value)}
                  label="餐廳"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">全部餐廳</MenuItem>
                  {restaurants.map((restaurant) => (
                    <MenuItem key={restaurant.id} value={restaurant.id}>
                      {restaurant.name}
                    </MenuItem>
                  ))}
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={2}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>最低評分</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={minRating}
                  onChange={(e) => setMinRating(e.target.value)}
                  label="最低評分"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">不限</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="1">1 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="2">2 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="3">3 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="4">4 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="5">5 星</MenuItem>
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={3}>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
                <Button
                  variant="outlined"
                  startIcon={<FilterList />}
                  onClick={() => {/* 可以添加更多篩選選項 */}}
                >
                  更多篩選
                </Button>
                {(searchQuery || selectedRestaurant || minRating) && (
                  <Button
                    variant="text"
                    onClick={clearFilters}
                  >
                    清除
                  </Button>
                )}
              </Box>
            </Grid>
          </Grid>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 拜訪紀錄列表 */}
      <Card>
        <CardContent>
          <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
            拜訪紀錄 ({filteredVisits.length} 筆)
          </Typography>
          
          {filteredVisits.length > 0 ? (
            <List>
              {filteredVisits.map((visit, index) => {
                const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={visit.id}>
                    <ListItem
                      sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${visit.restaurantId}`)}
                    >
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <Restaurant />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1, mb: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="h6">
                              {restaurant?.name}
                            </Typography>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                            <Chip 
                              label={new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')} 
                              size="small" 
                              variant="outlined"
                            />
                          </Box>
                        }
                        secondary={
                          <Box>
                            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2, mb: 1 }}>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <AttachMoney sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                              </Typography>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                {restaurant?.address}
                              </Typography>
                            </Box>
                            {visit.comment && (
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                "{visit.comment}"
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                            {visit.dishes && visit.dishes.length > 0 && (
                              <Box sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                                {visit.dishes.map((dish) => (
                                  <Chip
                                    key={dish.id}
                                    label={`${dish.name} (NT$ ${dish.price})`}
                                    size="small"
                                    variant="outlined"
                                    sx={{ mr: 0.5, mb: 0.5 }}
                                  />
                                ))}
                              </Box>
                            )}
                          </Box>
                        }
                      />
                      <Box>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            // 可以添加編輯功能
                          }}
                        >
                          <Edit />
                        </IconButton>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleDeleteClick(visit.id);
                          }}
                          color="error"
                        >
                          <Delete />
                        </IconButton>
                      </Box>
                    </ListItem>
                    {index < filteredVisits.length - 1 && <Divider />}
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </List>
          ) : (
            <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
              <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
                沒有找到符合條件的拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                試試調整篩選條件或新增拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
            </Box>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個拜訪紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default VisitRecordsPage;
```

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Grid,
  Avatar,
  Rating,
  Chip,
  Button,
  TextField,
  FormControl,
  InputLabel,
  Select,
  MenuItem,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemAvatar,
  ListItemText,
  Divider,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  CalendarToday,
  AttachMoney,
  Star,
  Restaurant,
  FilterList,
  Delete,
  Edit,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

const VisitRecordsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { visitRecords, restaurants, deleteVisitRecord } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState('');
  const [minRating, setMinRating] = useState('');
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visitToDelete, setVisitToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // 篩選拜訪紀錄
  const filteredVisits = visitRecords.filter(visit => {
    const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
    if (!restaurant) return false;

    // 搜尋篩選
    if (searchQuery && !restaurant.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) &&
        !visit.comment?.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) {
      return false;
    }

    // 餐廳篩選
    if (selectedRestaurant && visit.restaurantId !== selectedRestaurant) {
      return false;
    }

    // 評分篩選
    if (minRating && visit.rating < Number(minRating)) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime());

  const handleDeleteClick = (visitId: string) => {
    setVisitToDelete(visitId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (visitToDelete) {
      deleteVisitRecord(visitToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setVisitToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchQuery('');
    setSelectedRestaurant('');
    setMinRating('');
  };

  const totalSpent = filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.totalSpent, 0);
  const averageRating = filteredVisits.length > 0 
    ? filteredVisits.reduce((sum, visit) => sum + visit.rating, 0) / filteredVisits.length 
    : 0;

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          查看和管理你的餐廳拜訪紀錄
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 統計資訊 */}
      <Grid container spacing={3} sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <CalendarToday />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總拜訪次數
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {filteredVisits.length}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'success.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <AttachMoney />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    總花費
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    NT$ {totalSpent.toLocaleString()}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
        
        <Grid xs={12} sm={4}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'warning.main', mr: 2 }}>
                  <Star />
                </Avatar>
                <Box>
                  <Typography color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                    平均評分
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="h4">
                    {averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 搜尋和篩選 */}
      <Card sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <CardContent>
          <Grid container spacing={2} alignItems="center">
            <Grid xs={12} md={4}>
              <TextField
                fullWidth
                placeholder="搜尋餐廳或備註..."
                value={searchQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                InputProps={{
                  startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
                }}
              />
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={3}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>餐廳</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={selectedRestaurant}
                  onChange={(e) => setSelectedRestaurant(e.target.value)}
                  label="餐廳"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">全部餐廳</MenuItem>
                  {restaurants.map((restaurant) => (
                    <MenuItem key={restaurant.id} value={restaurant.id}>
                      {restaurant.name}
                    </MenuItem>
                  ))}
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={2}>
              <FormControl fullWidth>
                <InputLabel>最低評分</InputLabel>
                <Select
                  value={minRating}
                  onChange={(e) => setMinRating(e.target.value)}
                  label="最低評分"
                >
                  <MenuItem value="">不限</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="1">1 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="2">2 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="3">3 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="4">4 星以上</MenuItem>
                  <MenuItem value="5">5 星</MenuItem>
                </Select>
              </FormControl>
            </Grid>
            <Grid xs={12} md={3}>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 1 }}>
                <Button
                  variant="outlined"
                  startIcon={<FilterList />}
                  onClick={() => {/* 可以添加更多篩選選項 */}}
                >
                  更多篩選
                </Button>
                {(searchQuery || selectedRestaurant || minRating) && (
                  <Button
                    variant="text"
                    onClick={clearFilters}
                  >
                    清除
                  </Button>
                )}
              </Box>
            </Grid>
          </Grid>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 拜訪紀錄列表 */}
      <Card>
        <CardContent>
          <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
            拜訪紀錄 ({filteredVisits.length} 筆)
          </Typography>
          
          {filteredVisits.length > 0 ? (
            <List>
              {filteredVisits.map((visit, index) => {
                const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === visit.restaurantId);
                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={visit.id}>
                    <ListItem
                      sx={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
                      onClick={() => navigate(`/restaurants/${visit.restaurantId}`)}
                    >
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <Restaurant />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1, mb: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="h6">
                              {restaurant?.name}
                            </Typography>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                            <Chip 
                              label={new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')} 
                              size="small" 
                              variant="outlined"
                            />
                          </Box>
                        }
                        secondary={
                          <Box>
                            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2, mb: 1 }}>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <AttachMoney sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                              </Typography>
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                <Restaurant sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                                {restaurant?.address}
                              </Typography>
                            </Box>
                            {visit.comment && (
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                "{visit.comment}"
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                            {visit.dishes && visit.dishes.length > 0 && (
                              <Box sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                                {visit.dishes.map((dish) => (
                                  <Chip
                                    key={dish.id}
                                    label={`${dish.name} (NT$ ${dish.price})`}
                                    size="small"
                                    variant="outlined"
                                    sx={{ mr: 0.5, mb: 0.5 }}
                                  />
                                ))}
                              </Box>
                            )}
                          </Box>
                        }
                      />
                      <Box>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            // 可以添加編輯功能
                          }}
                        >
                          <Edit />
                        </IconButton>
                        <IconButton
                          size="small"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleDeleteClick(visit.id);
                          }}
                          color="error"
                        >
                          <Delete />
                        </IconButton>
                      </Box>
                    </ListItem>
                    {index < filteredVisits.length - 1 && <Divider />}
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </List>
          ) : (
            <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
              <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
                沒有找到符合條件的拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
                試試調整篩選條件或新增拜訪紀錄
              </Typography>
            </Box>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個拜訪紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default VisitRecordsPage;
```
