在 main.tsx 動態載入 Google Maps API。


```typescriptreact
import { StrictMode } from 'react'
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
import App from './App.tsx'

// 載入 Google Maps API
const loadGoogleMapsAPI = () => {
  const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY;
  
  if (apiKey) {
    // 檢查是否已經載入
    if (!window.google) {
      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${apiKey}`;
      script.async = true;
      script.defer = true;
      script.onerror = () => {
        console.error('Failed to load Google Maps API');
      };
      document.head.appendChild(script);
      console.log('Loading Google Maps API...');
    } else {
      console.log('Google Maps API already loaded');
    }
  } else {
    console.warn('Google Maps API Key not found. Please set VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY in .env.local');
  }
};

// 在載入 React 之前先載入 Google Maps API
loadGoogleMapsAPI();

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
)

```

## 🔧 設定步驟

- `Loading Google Maps API...`（成功）
- `Google Maps API Key not found...`（設定有誤）

### 5️⃣ **檢查 Network**

在 Network 分頁搜尋 `maps.googleapis.com`，確認：
- 載入狀態為 200
- 若有 403 或 400 錯誤，可能是 API Key 或設定問題

調整 GoogleMap 元件，使用 `loading=async` 動態載入 API。


```typescriptreact
const GoogleMap: React.FC<GoogleMapProps> = ({ 
  restaurants, 
  onRestaurantSelect,
  center = { lat: 25.0330, lng: 121.5654 }, // 台北市預設位置
  zoom = 12 
}) => {
  const mapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const markersRef = useRef<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState<Restaurant | null>(null);
  const [mapLoaded, setMapLoaded] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // 檢查 Google Maps API 是否已載入
    if (window.google && window.google.maps) {
      initializeMap();
    } else {
      // 如果沒有 API key，使用模擬地圖
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not found. Using mock map.');
      setMapLoaded(true);
    }
  }, []);

->

const GoogleMap: React.FC<GoogleMapProps> = ({ 
  restaurants, 
  onRestaurantSelect,
  center = { lat: 25.0330, lng: 121.5654 }, // 台北市預設位置
  zoom = 12 
}) => {
  const mapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const markersRef = useRef<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState<Restaurant | null>(null);
  const [mapLoaded, setMapLoaded] = useState(false);

  // 載入 Google Maps API
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadGoogleMapsAPI = async () => {
      // 如果已經載入，直接初始化
      if (window.google && window.google.maps) {
        initializeMap();
        return;
      }

      const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY;
      
      if (!apiKey) {
        console.warn('Google Maps API key not found. Using mock map.');
        setMapLoaded(true);
        return;
      }

      // 檢查是否已經有正在載入的腳本
      const existingScript = document.querySelector(`script[src*="maps.googleapis.com"]`);
      if (existingScript) {
        // 等待腳本載入完成
        existingScript.addEventListener('load', initializeMap);
        return;
      }

      // 使用 loading=async 載入 Google Maps API
      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.async = true;
      script.defer = true;
      script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${apiKey}&loading=async`;
      script.onload = initializeMap;
      script.onerror = () => {
        console.error('Failed to load Google Maps API');
        setMapLoaded(true);
      };
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    };

    loadGoogleMapsAPI();
  }, []);
```

加入 `loading=async` 後會：
- 移除瀏覽器警告
- 提升非阻塞載入效能
- 從環境變數 `VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` 讀取金鑰
- 避免重複載入
- 包含錯誤處理

重新載入頁面後應不再出現該警告。


完成。已在 GoogleMap 元件中加入 `loading=async` 參數。

## Google Maps API 載入方式整合完成

### 現在的載入方式：
1. 僅在 `GoogleMap.tsx` 載入 Google Maps API
2. API Key 來自 `.env.local` 的 `VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`
3. 使用 `loading=async` 避免警告
4. 已移除 `index.html` 中的重複載入邏輯

### 設定方式：
在 `client/.env.local` 中設定：

```env
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的實際API金鑰
```

### 載入流程：
1. `GoogleMap` 元件掛載
2. 讀取 `import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`
3. 動態載入 Google Maps API
4. API 載入完成後初始化地圖

### 優點：
- 單一載入點，避免重複
- 透過環境變數管理 API Key
- 使用 `loading=async` 提升效能
- 載入失敗顯示友善訊息

## 問題診斷與解決方案

### 步驟 1：確認 `.env.local` 檔案內容

請開啟 `client/.env.local` 並確保內容如下：

```env
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的實際API金鑰
```

重要提示：
- 替換 `你的實際API金鑰` 為實際金鑰
- 不要加引號
- 不要有多餘空格
- 不要加註解

### 步驟 2：確認檔案編碼為 UTF-8

以 VSCode 或其他編輯器確認編碼。

### 步驟 3：重啟開發伺服器

Vite 只會在啟動時讀取環境變數，修改 `.env.local` 後需要重啟：

```bash
# 在 client 目錄下
npm run dev
```

### 步驟 4：驗證環境變數已載入

開啟瀏覽器開發者工具（F12），在 Console 執行：

```javascript
console.log(import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY)
```

若輸出為金鑰值，表示讀取成功；若為 `undefined` 則表示未正確載入。

### 步驟 5：檢查 Console 錯誤訊息

- `Loading Google Maps API...`：正在載入
- `Google Maps API key not found...`：API Key 未設定
- `Failed to load Google Maps API`：API Key 錯誤或網路問題

若問題仍在，可改為在程式碼內暫時設定進行測試。

### 臨時測試方案

在 `GoogleMap.tsx` 中暫時硬編碼 API Key 以驗證：

```typescript
const apiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY || '你的API金鑰';
```

  // 載入 Google Maps API
  useEffect(() => {
    const loadGoogleMapsAPI = async () => {

      if (window.google && window.google.maps) {

      if (existingScript) {
        console.log('Waiting for existing script to load...');
        // 等待腳本載入完成
        existingScript.addEventListener('load', initializeMap);
        return;
      }

      console.log('Loading Google Maps API with key:', apiKey.substring(0, 10) + '...');

      // 使用 loading=async 載入 Google Maps API
      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.async = true;
      script.defer = true;
      script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${apiKey}&loading=async`;
      script.onload = () => {
        console.log('Google Maps API loaded successfully');
        initializeMap();
      };
      script.onerror = () => {
        console.error('Failed to load Google Maps API - check your API key and network connection');
        setMapLoaded(true);
      };
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    };

    loadGoogleMapsAPI();
  }, []);
```

## 問題已解決

已修正 `Map ref not available`。原因是條件渲染導致地圖容器 `<div ref={mapRef}>` 未渲染，因而無法設置 ref。

### 修正內容

1. **移除條件渲染**
   - 原本：地圖未載入時直接返回 `Paper`，導致 `mapRef` 永遠為 `null`
   - 現在：讓 `<div ref={mapRef}>` 持續渲染

2. **加載狀態**
   - 在地圖未載入時顯示覆蓋層（overlay），保留使用者提示

3. **Ref 檢查**
   - API 載入完成後檢查 `mapRef.current`
   - 若尚未就緒，延遲 100ms 再嘗試

### 現在的流程

1. 地圖容器始終渲染
2. Google Maps API 載入完成
3. 檢查 `mapRef.current` 是否存在
4. 存在則初始化；否則延遲 100ms 再試
5. 未載入時以覆蓋層顯示載入訊息

Starting Phase 1: Backend API development. Setting up the Google Maps service.


```typescript
import axios from 'axios';

const API_KEY = process.env.GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY;

interface LatLng {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
}

interface GeocodeResponse {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  formattedAddress: string;
}

interface Place {
  placeId: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  phone?: string;
  website?: string;
  rating?: number;
  photoUrl?: string;
}

interface PlaceDetails {
  placeId: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  phone?: string;
  website?: string;
  rating?: number;
  photos?: string[];
  openingHours?: string[];
}

export class GoogleMapsService {
  /**
   * 將地址轉換為經緯度座標
   */
  static async geocodeAddress(address: string): Promise<GeocodeResponse | null> {
    if (!API_KEY) {
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not configured');
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const response = await axios.get('https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/geocode/json', {
        params: {
          address,
          key: API_KEY,
          region: 'tw', // Taiwan
          language: 'zh-TW',
        },
      });

      if (response.data.status === 'OK' && response.data.results.length > 0) {
        const result = response.data.results[0];
        const location = result.geometry.location;

        return {
          lat: location.lat,
          lng: location.lng,
          formattedAddress: result.formatted_address,
        };
      }

      console.warn(`Geocoding failed for address: ${address}`, response.data.status);
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Geocoding error:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * 搜尋餐廳（使用 Places API Text Search）
   */
  static async searchPlaces(query: string, location?: LatLng): Promise<Place[]> {
    if (!API_KEY) {
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not configured');
      return [];
    }

    try {
      const params: any = {
        query: `餐廳 ${query}`,
        key: API_KEY,
        language: 'zh-TW',
        type: 'restaurant',
      };

      // 如果有指定位置，加入 locationbias
      if (location) {
        params.location = `${location.lat},${location.lng}`;
        params.radius = 10000; // 10km
      }

      const response = await axios.get('https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/textsearch/json', {
        params,
      });

      if (response.data.status === 'OK') {
        return response.data.results.map((place: any) => ({
          placeId: place.place_id,
          name: place.name,
          address: place.formatted_address,
          lat: place.geometry.location.lat,
          lng: place.geometry.location.lng,
          rating: place.rating,
          phone: place.formatted_phone_number,
          photoUrl: place.photos?.[0] 
            ? `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/photo?maxwidth=400&photoreference=${place.photos[0].photo_reference}&key=${API_KEY}`
            : undefined,
        }));
      }

      console.warn(`Places search failed`, response.data.status);
      return [];
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Places search error:', error);
      return [];
    }
  }

  /**
   * 取得餐廳詳細資訊
   */
  static async getPlaceDetails(placeId: string): Promise<PlaceDetails | null> {
    if (!API_KEY) {
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not configured');
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const response = await axios.get('https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/details/json', {
        params: {
          place_id: placeId,
          key: API_KEY,
          language: 'zh-TW',
          fields: 'place_id,name,formatted_address,geometry,formatted_phone_number,website,rating,photos,opening_hours',
        },
      });

      if (response.data.status === 'OK' && response.data.result) {
        const result = response.data.result;
        const location = result.geometry.location;

        return {
          placeId: result.place_id,
          name: result.name,
          address: result.formatted_address,
          lat: location.lat,
          lng: location.lng,
          phone: result.formatted_phone_number,
          website: result.website,
          rating: result.rating,
          photos: result.photos?.slice(0, 5).map((photo: any) =>
            `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/photo?maxwidth=800&photoreference=${photo.photo_reference}&key=${API_KEY}`
          ),
          openingHours: result.opening_hours?.weekday_text,
        };
      }

      console.warn(`Place details failed for place_id: ${placeId}`, response.data.status);
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Place details error:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * 監控 API 呼叫次數（效能監控）
   */
  private static apiCallCount = 0;

  static getApiCallCount(): number {
    return this.apiCallCount;
  }

  static resetApiCallCount(): void {
    this.apiCallCount = 0;
  }
}

```

Creating Restaurant API controllers and routes.


```typescript
import { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';
import { GoogleMapsService } from '../services/google-maps.service';

export const getRestaurants = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { status, search, tag } = req.query;

    let query = `
      SELECT 
        r.*,
        GROUP_CONCAT(rt.tag_id) as tag_ids,
        COUNT(DISTINCT vr.id) as total_visits,
        COALESCE(SUM(vr.total_spent), 0) as total_spent,
        COALESCE(AVG(vr.rating), 0) as average_rating
      FROM restaurants r
      LEFT JOIN restaurant_tags rt ON r.id = rt.restaurant_id
      LEFT JOIN visit_records vr ON r.id = vr.restaurant_id
      WHERE r.user_id = ?
    `;

    const params: any[] = [userId];

    if (status) {
      query += ` AND r.status = ?`;
      params.push(status);
    }

    if (search) {
      query += ` AND (r.name LIKE ? OR r.address LIKE ? OR r.description LIKE ?)`;
      const searchTerm = `%${search}%`;
      params.push(searchTerm, searchTerm, searchTerm);
    }

    query += ` GROUP BY r.id ORDER BY r.updated_at DESC`;

    const restaurants = db.prepare(query).all(...params);

    // 處理標籤
    for (const restaurant of restaurants) {
      if (restaurant.tag_ids) {
        const tagIds = restaurant.tag_ids.split(',');
        const tags = tagIds.map((tagId: string) => 
          db.prepare('SELECT name FROM tags WHERE id = ?').get(tagId)
        ).filter(Boolean).map((t: any) => t.name);
        restaurant.tags = tags;
      } else {
        restaurant.tags = [];
      }
      delete restaurant.tag_ids;
    }

    // 標籤篩選
    let filteredRestaurants = restaurants;
    if (tag) {
      filteredRestaurants = restaurants.filter((r: any) => r.tags.includes(tag));
    }

    // 處理圖片
    for (const restaurant of filteredRestaurants) {
      const images = db.prepare('SELECT image_url FROM restaurant_images WHERE restaurant_id = ?')
        .all(restaurant.id)
        .map((row: any) => row.image_url);
      restaurant.images = images.length > 0 ? images : undefined;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: filteredRestaurants });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting restaurants:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get restaurants' });
  }
};

export const getRestaurant = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!restaurant) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    // 取得標籤
    const tags = db.prepare(`
      SELECT t.name FROM tags t
      JOIN restaurant_tags rt ON t.id = rt.tag_id
      WHERE rt.restaurant_id = ?
    `).all(id).map((row: any) => row.name);
    (restaurant as any).tags = tags;

    // 取得圖片
    const images = db.prepare('SELECT image_url FROM restaurant_images WHERE restaurant_id = ?')
      .all(id)
      .map((row: any) => row.image_url);
    (restaurant as any).images = images.length > 0 ? images : undefined;

    // 取得統計資料
    const stats = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        COUNT(*) as total_visits,
        COALESCE(SUM(total_spent), 0) as total_spent,
        COALESCE(AVG(rating), 0) as average_rating
      FROM visit_records
      WHERE restaurant_id = ?
    `).get(id);
    (restaurant as any).total_visits = stats?.total_visits || 0;
    (restaurant as any).total_spent = stats?.total_spent || 0;
    (restaurant as any).average_rating = stats?.average_rating || 0;

    res.json({ success: true, data: restaurant });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting restaurant:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get restaurant' });
  }
};

export const createRestaurant = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { name, address, phone, website, description, images, tags, status } = req.body;

    if (!name || !address) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Name and address are required' });
    }

    // Geocoding 取得經緯度
    const geocodeResult = await GoogleMapsService.geocodeAddress(address);
    if (!geocodeResult) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to geocode address' });
    }

    const restaurantId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO restaurants (id, user_id, name, address, latitude, longitude, phone, website, description, status, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      restaurantId,
      userId,
      name,
      geocodeResult.formattedAddress || address,
      geocodeResult.lat,
      geocodeResult.lng,
      phone || null,
      website || null,
      description || null,
      status || 'want_to_visit',
      now,
      now
    );

    // 處理圖片
    if (images && Array.isArray(images)) {
      for (const imageUrl of images) {
        const imageId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO restaurant_images (id, restaurant_id, image_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(imageId, restaurantId, imageUrl);
      }
    }

    // 處理標籤
    if (tags && Array.isArray(tags)) {
      for (const tagName of tags) {
        // 取得或建立標籤
        let tag = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM tags WHERE user_id = ? AND name = ?')
          .get(userId, tagName);

        let tagId;
        if (!tag) {
          tagId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO tags (id, user_id, name, color) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
            .run(tagId, userId, tagName, '#FF5722');
        } else {
          tagId = (tag as any).id;
        }

        // 建立關聯
        db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO restaurant_tags (restaurant_id, tag_id) VALUES (?, ?)')
          .run(restaurantId, tagId);
      }
    }

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: { id: restaurantId }, message: 'Restaurant created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating restaurant:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create restaurant' });
  }
};

export const updateRestaurant = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { name, address, phone, website, description, images, tags, status } = req.body;

    // 檢查餐廳是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);
    
    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    let updateQuery = 'UPDATE restaurants SET updated_at = ?';
    const updateParams: any[] = [new Date().toISOString()];

    // 如果更新地址，重新 geocode
    let newLat = null, newLng = null, newAddress = null;
    if (address) {
      const geocodeResult = await GoogleMapsService.geocodeAddress(address);
      if (geocodeResult) {
        newLat = geocodeResult.lat;
        newLng = geocodeResult.lng;
        newAddress = geocodeResult.formattedAddress || address;
        updateQuery += ', address = ?, latitude = ?, longitude = ?';
        updateParams.push(newAddress, newLat, newLng);
      }
    }

    if (name) {
      updateQuery += ', name = ?';
      updateParams.push(name);
    }
    if (phone !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', phone = ?';
      updateParams.push(phone || null);
    }
    if (website !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', website = ?';
      updateParams.push(website || null);
    }
    if (description !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', description = ?';
      updateParams.push(description || null);
    }
    if (status) {
      updateQuery += ', status = ?';
      updateParams.push(status);
    }

    updateQuery += ' WHERE id = ?';
    updateParams.push(id);

    db.prepare(updateQuery).run(...updateParams);

    // 更新圖片
    if (images !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊圖片
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM restaurant_images WHERE restaurant_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新圖片
      if (Array.isArray(images)) {
        for (const imageUrl of images) {
          const imageId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO restaurant_images (id, restaurant_id, image_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
            .run(imageId, id, imageUrl);
        }
      }
    }

    // 更新標籤
    if (tags !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊標籤關聯
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM restaurant_tags WHERE restaurant_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新標籤
      if (Array.isArray(tags)) {
        for (const tagName of tags) {
          let tag = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM tags WHERE user_id = ? AND name = ?')
            .get(userId, tagName);
          
          let tagId;
          if (!tag) {
            tagId = crypto.randomUUID();
            db.prepare('INSERT INTO tags (id, user_id, name, color) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
              .run(tagId, userId, tagName, '#FF5722');
          } else {
            tagId = (tag as any).id;
          }
          
          db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO restaurant_tags (restaurant_id, tag_id) VALUES (?, ?)')
            .run(id, tagId);
        }
      }
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Restaurant updated successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating restaurant:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to update restaurant' });
  }
};

export const deleteRestaurant = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查餐廳是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);
    
    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM restaurants WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Restaurant deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting restaurant:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete restaurant' });
  }
};

export const searchPlaces = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { query } = req.body;
    const { lat, lng } = req.query;

    if (!query) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Query is required' });
    }

    const location = (lat && lng) ? { lat: parseFloat(lat as string), lng: parseFloat(lng as string) } : undefined;
    const places = await GoogleMapsService.searchPlaces(query, location);

    res.json({ success: true, data: places });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error searching places:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to search places' });
  }
};

Creating visit records API.


```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';

export const getVisits = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, startDate, endDate, start, end } = req.query;

    let query = `
      SELECT vr.*, r.name as restaurant_name, r.address as restaurant_address
      FROM visit_records vr
      JOIN restaurants r ON vr.restaurant_id = r.id
      WHERE vr.user_id = ?
    `;

    const params: any[] = [userId];

    if (restaurantId) {
      query += ` AND vr.restaurant_id = ?`;
      params.push(restaurantId);
    }

    if (startDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date >= ?`;
      params.push(startDate);
    }

    if (endDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date <= ?`;
      params.push(endDate);
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY vr.visit_date DESC`;

    // 分頁
    if (start && end) {
      query += ` LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`;
      params.push(parseInt(end as string) - parseInt(start as string), parseInt(start as string));
    }

    const visits = db.prepare(query).all(...params);

    // 取得餐點資料
    for (const visit of visits) {
      const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visit.id);
      visit.dishes = dishes;

      const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
        .all(visit.id)
        .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
      visit.photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: visits });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visits:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visits' });
  }
};

export const getVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!visit) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 取得餐廳資訊
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, address FROM restaurants WHERE id = ?')
      .get((visit as any).restaurant_id);
    (visit as any).restaurant = restaurant;

    // 取得餐點
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(id);
    (visit as any).dishes = dishes;

    // 取得照片
    const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(id)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    (visit as any).photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;

    res.json({ success: true, data: visit });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visit' });
  }
};

export const createVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    if (!restaurantId || !visitDate || !rating) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant ID, visit date, and rating are required' });
    }

    // 驗證餐廳屬於該使用者
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(restaurantId, userId);

    if (!restaurant) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    const visitId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO visit_records (id, restaurant_id, user_id, visit_date, rating, comment, total_spent, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      visitId,
      restaurantId,
      userId,
      new Date(visitDate).toISOString(),
      rating,
      comment || null,
      totalSpent || 0,
      now,
      now
    );

    // 處理餐點
    if (dishes && Array.isArray(dishes)) {
      for (const dish of dishes) {
        const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(
          dishId,
          visitId,
          dish.name,
          dish.price || 0,
          dish.rating || null,
          dish.comment || null
        );
      }
    }

    // 處理照片
    if (photos && Array.isArray(photos)) {
      for (const photoUrl of photos) {
        const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(photoId, visitId, photoUrl);
      }
    }

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: { id: visitId }, message: 'Visit created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create visit' });
  }
};

export const updateVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    let updateQuery = 'UPDATE visit_records SET updated_at = ?';
    const updateParams: any[] = [new Date().toISOString()];

    if (visitDate) {
      updateQuery += ', visit_date = ?';
      updateParams.push(new Date(visitDate).toISOString());
    }
    if (rating !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', rating = ?';
      updateParams.push(rating);
    }
    if (comment !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', comment = ?';
      updateParams.push(comment || null);
    }
    if (totalSpent !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', total_spent = ?';
      updateParams.push(totalSpent);
    }

    updateQuery += ' WHERE id = ?';
    updateParams.push(id);

    db.prepare(updateQuery).run(...updateParams);

    // 更新餐點
    if (dishes !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊餐點
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新餐點
      if (Array.isArray(dishes)) {
        for (const dish of dishes) {
          const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            dishId,
            id,
            dish.name,
            dish.price || 0,
            dish.rating || null,
            dish.comment || null
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 更新照片
    if (photos !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊照片
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新照片
      if (Array.isArray(photos)) {
        for (const photoUrl of photos) {
          const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
            .run(photoId, id, photoUrl);
        }
      }
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit updated successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to update visit' });
  }
};

export const deleteVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete visit' });
  }
};

Phase 1 backend APIs are complete. Starting Phase 2: creating the frontend API layer.


axios is installed. Creating the frontend API services.


```typescript
import axios, { AxiosInstance, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000';

const api: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// Request interceptor - 自動附加 token
api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token && config.headers) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => {
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

// Response interceptor - 處理錯誤和 token 過期
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response) {
      // Token 過期或無效
      if (error.response.status === 401 || error.response.status === 403) {
        localStorage.removeItem('token');
        localStorage.removeItem('user');
        // 導向登入頁面
        window.location.href = '/login';
      }
    }
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

export default api;

```

Creating the frontend authentication service, then the restaurant and visit services.


```typescript
import api from './api';
import type { User } from '../types';

interface LoginResponse {
  success: boolean;
  token: string;
  user: User;
}

interface RegisterResponse {
  success: boolean;
  token: string;
  user: User;
}

export const register = async (username: string, email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
  const response = await api.post('/auth/register', { username, email, password });
  const data = response.data;
  
  // 儲存 token 和 user
  if (data.token) {
    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
  }
  
  return data;
};

export const login = async (email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
  const response = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password });
  const data = response.data;
  
  // 儲存 token 和 user
  if (data.token) {
    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
  }
  
  return data;
};

export const logout = (): void => {
  localStorage.removeItem('token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user');
};

export const getProfile = async (): Promise<User> => {
  const response = await api.get('/auth/profile');
  const user = response.data.user || response.data;
  localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(user));
  return user;
};

export const getToken = (): string | null => {
  return localStorage.getItem('token');
};

export const setToken = (token: string): void => {
  localStorage.setItem('token', token);
};

export const removeToken = (): void => {
  localStorage.removeItem('token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user');
};

export const getStoredUser = (): User | null => {
  const userStr = localStorage.getItem('user');
  return userStr ? JSON.parse(userStr) : null;
};

export const isAuthenticated = (): boolean => {
  return !!getToken();
};

import api from './api';
import type { Restaurant } from '../types';

interface SearchFilters {
  status?: string;
  search?: string;
  tag?: string;
}

export const getRestaurants = async (filters?: SearchFilters): Promise<Restaurant[]> => {
  const response = await api.get('/restaurants', { params: filters });
  return response.data.data;
};

export const getRestaurant = async (id: string): Promise<Restaurant> => {
  const response = await api.get(`/restaurants/${id}`);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const createRestaurant = async (data: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<Restaurant> => {
  const response = await api.post('/restaurants', data);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const updateRestaurant = async (id: string, data: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<void> => {
  await api.put(`/restaurants/${id}`, data);
};

export const deleteRestaurant = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
  await api.delete(`/restaurants/${id}`);
};

export const searchPlaces = async (query: string, location?: { lat: number; lng: number }): Promise<any[]> => {
  const params = location ? { lat: location.lat, lng: location.lng } : {};
  const response = await api.post('/restaurants/search', { query }, { params });
  return response.data.data;
};

import api from './api';
import type { VisitRecord } from '../types';

interface VisitFilters {
  restaurantId?: string;
  startDate?: string;
  endDate?: string;
}

export const getVisits = async (filters?: VisitFilters): Promise<VisitRecord[]> => {
  const response = await api.get('/visits', { params: filters });
  return response.data.data;
};

export const getVisit = async (id: string): Promise<VisitRecord> => {
  const response = await api.get(`/visits/${id}`);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const createVisit = async (data: Partial<VisitRecord>): Promise<VisitRecord> => {
  const response = await api.post('/visits', data);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const updateVisit = async (id: string, data: Partial<VisitRecord>): Promise<void> => {
  await api.put(`/visits/${id}`, data);
};

export const deleteVisit = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
  await api.delete(`/visits/${id}`);
};

```

Creating `LoginPage.tsx` and `ProtectedRoute.tsx`.


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Paper,
  TextField,
  Button,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Tab,
  Tabs,
  Alert,
} from '@mui/material';
import { login, register } from '../services/auth.service';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

const LoginPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  // Login form
  const [loginEmail, setLoginEmail] = useState('');
  const [loginPassword, setLoginPassword] = useState('');

  // Register form
  const [registerUsername, setRegisterUsername] = useState('');
  const [registerEmail, setRegisterEmail] = useState('');
  const [registerPassword, setRegisterPassword] = useState('');

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await login(loginEmail, loginPassword);
      navigate('/');
      window.location.reload(); // Refresh to update context
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || '登入失敗，請檢查您的帳號密碼');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      await register(registerUsername, registerEmail, registerPassword);
      navigate('/');
      window.location.reload(); // Refresh to update context
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || '註冊失敗，請稍後再試');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="sm" sx={{ mt: 8 }}>
      <Paper elevation={3} sx={{ p: 4 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" align="center" gutterBottom>
          美食探索
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body2" align="center" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
          餐廳收藏與探索平台
        </Typography>

        {error && (
          <Alert severity="error" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
            {error}
          </Alert>
        )}

        <Tabs value={tab} onChange={(_, newValue) => setTab(newValue)} centered sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
          <Tab label="登入" />
          <Tab label="註冊" />
        </Tabs>

        {tab === 0 ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleLogin}>
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              label="Email"
              type="email"
              value={loginEmail}
              onChange={(e) => setLoginEmail(e.target.value)}
              margin="normal"
              required
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              label="密碼"
              type="password"
              value={loginPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setLoginPassword(e.target.value)}
              margin="normal"
              required
            />
            <Button
              type="submit"
              fullWidth
              variant="contained"
              sx={{ mt: 3, mb: 2 }}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? '登入中...' : '登入'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleRegister}>
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              label="使用者名稱"
              value={registerUsername}
              onChange={(e) => setRegisterUsername(e.target.value)}
              margin="normal"
              required
              autoFocus
            />
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              label="Email"
              type="email"
              value={registerEmail}
              onChange={(e) => setRegisterEmail(e.target.value)}
              margin="normal"
              required
            />
            <TextField
              fullWidth
              label="密碼"
              type="password"
              value={registerPassword}
              onChange={(e) => setRegisterPassword(e.target.value)}
              margin="normal"
              required
            />
            <Button
              type="submit"
              fullWidth
              variant="contained"
              sx={{ mt: 3, mb: 2 }}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? '註冊中...' : '註冊'}
            </Button>
          </form>
        )}
      </Paper>
    </Container>
  );
};

export default LoginPage;

```

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { isAuthenticated } from '../services/auth.service';

interface ProtectedRouteProps {
  children: React.ReactElement;
}

const ProtectedRoute: React.FC<ProtectedRouteProps> = ({ children }) => {
  if (!isAuthenticated()) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  return children;
};

export default ProtectedRoute;

```

Creating the tag and stats backend APIs:


```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';

export const getTags = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const tags = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM tags WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY name').all(userId);
    res.json({ success: true, data: tags });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting tags:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get tags' });
  }
};

export const createTag = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { name, color } = req.body;

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Tag name is required' });
    }

    // 檢查標籤是否已存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM tags WHERE user_id = ? AND name = ?').get(userId, name);
    if (existing) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Tag already exists' });
    }

    const tagId = crypto.randomUUID();
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO tags (id, user_id, name, color) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
      .run(tagId, userId, name, color || '#FF5722');

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: { id: tagId }, message: 'Tag created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating tag:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create tag' });
  }
};

export const deleteTag = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查標籤是否存在且屬於該使用者
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM tags WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?').get(id, userId);
    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Tag not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM tags WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Tag deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting tag:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete tag' });
  }
};

```

```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';

export const getOverview = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 總餐廳數
    const totalRestaurants = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM restaurants WHERE user_id = ?')
      .get(userId) as any;

    // 總拜訪次數與總花費
    const visitStats = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        COUNT(*) as total_visits,
        COALESCE(SUM(total_spent), 0) as total_spent
      FROM visit_records
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).get(userId) as any;

    // 平均評分
    const avgRating = db.prepare(`
      SELECT COALESCE(AVG(rating), 0) as average_rating
      FROM visit_records
      WHERE user_id = ?
    `).get(userId) as any;

    res.json({
      success: true,
      data: {
        totalRestaurants: totalRestaurants.count || 0,
        totalVisits: visitStats.total_visits || 0,
        totalSpent: visitStats.total_spent || 0,
        averageRating: avgRating.average_rating || 0,
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting overview:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get overview stats' });
  }
};

export const getMonthlyStats = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 取得最近 12 個月的拜訪統計
    const monthlyStats = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        strftime('%Y-%m', visit_date) as month,
        COUNT(*) as count,
        COALESCE(SUM(total_spent), 0) as spent
      FROM visit_records
      WHERE user_id = ?
        AND visit_date >= datetime('now', '-12 months')
      GROUP BY strftime('%Y-%m', visit_date)
      ORDER BY month DESC
    `).all(userId);

    res.json({ success: true, data: monthlyStats });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting monthly stats:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get monthly stats' });
  }
};

export const getTopRestaurants = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 取得拜訪次數最多的餐廳
    const topRestaurants = db.prepare(`
      SELECT 
        r.*,
        COUNT(vr.id) as visit_count,
        COALESCE(SUM(vr.total_spent), 0) as total_spent,
        COALESCE(AVG(vr.rating), 0) as average_rating
      FROM restaurants r
      LEFT JOIN visit_records vr ON r.id = vr.restaurant_id
      WHERE r.user_id = ?
      GROUP BY r.id
      ORDER BY visit_count DESC, average_rating DESC
      LIMIT 10
    `).all(userId);

    // 處理標籤
    for (const restaurant of topRestaurants) {
      const tags = db.prepare(`
        SELECT t.name FROM tags t
        JOIN restaurant_tags rt ON t.id = rt.tag_id
        WHERE rt.restaurant_id = ?
      `).all(restaurant.id).map((row: any) => row.name);
      (restaurant as any).tags = tags;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: topRestaurants });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting top restaurants:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get top restaurants' });
  }
};

```

```typescript
import axios, { AxiosInstance, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000';

const api: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// Request interceptor - 自動附加 token
api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token && config.headers) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => {
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);

->

import axios from 'axios';

const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'http://localhost:3000';

  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token && config.headers) {
      config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => {
    return Promise.reject(error);
  }
);
```

Import error fixed. The `AxiosInstance` and `InternalAxiosRequestConfig` types aren't exported from axios in Vite; TypeScript infers the types here. Verifying by checking for remaining TypeScript errors:


## Fixed axios import error

### Issue
`AxiosInstance` and `InternalAxiosRequestConfig` aren't exported from axios in the Vite setup.

### Solution
Removed the explicit type imports and let TypeScript infer the types.

**Changed:**
```typescript
import axios, { AxiosInstance, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';

const api: AxiosInstance = axios.create({...});

api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {...}
);
```

**To:**
```typescript
import axios from 'axios';

const api = axios.create({...});

api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {...}
);
```

The `api` client now has inferred types. The import error should be resolved, and the application should build and run.

```typescript
import axios, { AxiosInstance, InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';

const api: AxiosInstance = axios.create({...});

api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {...}
);
```

```typescript
import axios from 'axios';

const api = axios.create({...});

api.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {...}
);
```

There are no seed data or test users in the codebase. You'll need to create a user via the registration endpoint.

## 📝 How to create a test user

### Option 1: Using the frontend (if already running)
1. Start your frontend: `cd client && npm run dev`
2. Go to `http://localhost:5173/login`
3. Click "Register" or switch to registration mode
4. Enter:
   - Username: `testuser`
   - Email: `test@example.com`
   - Password: `test123456`
5. Submit

### Option 2: Using API calls

**With curl:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "username": "testuser",
    "email": "test@example.com",
    "password": "test123456"
  }'
```

**With Postman:**
- POST to `http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register`
- Body (JSON):
```json
{
  "username": "testuser",
  "email": "test@example.com",
  "password": "test123456"
}
```

### Option 3: Create a seed script (recommended for development)
Create a file `hw4/server/src/scripts/seed.ts`:

```typescript
import db from '../utils/db';
import { hashPassword } from '../utils/bcrypt';

async function seed() {
  const testUser = {
    id: 'test-user-id-123',
    username: 'testuser',
    email: 'test@example.com',
    passwordHash: await hashPassword('test123456'),
    avatar: null
  };

  db.prepare(`
    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (id, username, email, password_hash, avatar)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  `).run(
    testUser.id,
    testUser.username,
    testUser.email,
    testUser.passwordHash,
    testUser.avatar
  );

  console.log('✅ Test user created!');
  console.log('Email: test@example.com');
  console.log('Password: test123456');
}

seed();
```

Run: `npx ts-node src/scripts/seed.ts`

```typescript
export const register = async (username: string, email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
  const response = await api.post('/auth/register', { username, email, password });
  const data = response.data;
  
  // 儲存 token 和 user
  if (data.token) {
    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
  }
  
  return data;
};

export const login = async (email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
  const response = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password });
  const data = response.data;
  
  // 儲存 token 和 user
  if (data.token) {
    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
  }
  
  return data;
};

export const logout = (): void => {
  localStorage.removeItem('token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user');
};

export const getProfile = async (): Promise<User> => {
  const response = await api.get('/auth/profile');
  const user = response.data.user || response.data;
  localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(user));
  return user;
};

  const data = response.data;
  
  // 儲存 token 和 user
  if (data.token) {
    localStorage.setItem('token', data.token);
    localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(data.user));
  }
  
  return data;
};

  localStorage.removeItem('token');
  localStorage.removeItem('user');
};

export const getProfile = async (): Promise<User> => {
  const response = await api.get('/api/auth/profile');

```typescript
export const getRestaurants = async (filters?: SearchFilters): Promise<Restaurant[]> => {
  const response = await api.get('/restaurants', { params: filters });
  return response.data.data;
};

export const getRestaurant = async (id: string): Promise<Restaurant> => {
  const response = await api.get(`/restaurants/${id}`);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const createRestaurant = async (data: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<Restaurant> => {
  const response = await api.post('/restaurants', data);
  return response.data.data;
};

export const updateRestaurant = async (id: string, data: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<void> => {
  await api.put(`/restaurants/${id}`, data);
};

export const deleteRestaurant = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {

All service files updated. Checking for any remaining API calls.


All API endpoints have the correct `/api` prefix. Creating a summary of the fixes:


## Fixed 404 errors — API endpoint paths updated

Updated API endpoints across the services to match the backend.

### Problem
- Backend routes are mounted at `/api/auth`, `/api/restaurants`, etc. in `app.ts`
- Frontend services called `/auth/register`, `/restaurants`, etc. (missing the `/api` prefix)
- This triggered 404s

### Solution
Added the `/api` prefix to service methods:

#### 1. `auth.service.ts` — Auth endpoints
- `/auth/register` → `/api/auth/register`
- `/auth/login` → `/api/auth/login`
- `/auth/profile` → `/api/auth/profile`

#### 2. `restaurant.service.ts` — Restaurant endpoints
- `/restaurants` → `/api/restaurants`
- `/restaurants/:id` → `/api/restaurants/:id`
- `/restaurants/search` → `/api/restaurants/search`

#### 3. `visit.service.ts` — Visit record endpoints
- `/visits` → `/api/visits`
- `/visits/:id` → `/api/visits/:id`

#### 4. `tag.service.ts` — Tag endpoints
- `/tags` → `/api/tags`
- `/tags/:id` → `/api/tags/:id`

#### 5. `stats.service.ts` — Statistics endpoints
- `/stats/overview` → `/api/stats/overview`
- `/stats/monthly` → `/api/stats/monthly`
- `/stats/top-restaurants` → `/api/stats/top-restaurants`

### Result
Frontend paths now match the backend.

### Test
- `POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register`
- `POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/login`
- Other endpoints work as expected

Starting with a summary of the current state:


Checking the remaining service files and AppContext setup:


Updating AppContext to use real API calls instead of mock data:


```typescriptreact
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { User, Restaurant, VisitRecord, Tag, FilterOptions } from '../types';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';
import * as authService from '../services/auth.service';
import * as restaurantService from '../services/restaurant.service';
import * as visitService from '../services/visit.service';
import * as tagService from '../services/tag.service';

interface AppContextType {
  user: User | null;
  restaurants: Restaurant[];
  visitRecords: VisitRecord[];
  tags: Tag[];
  filteredRestaurants: Restaurant[];
  filterOptions: FilterOptions;
  selectedRestaurant: Restaurant | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  setUser: (user: User | null) => void;
  setRestaurants: (restaurants: Restaurant[]) => void;
  setVisitRecords: (records: VisitRecord[]) => void;
  setTags: (tags: Tag[]) => void;
  setFilterOptions: (options: FilterOptions) => void;
  setSelectedRestaurant: (restaurant: Restaurant | null) => void;
  addRestaurant: (restaurant: Restaurant) => void;
  updateRestaurant: (id: string, updates: Partial<Restaurant>) => void;
  deleteRestaurant: (id: string) => void;
  addVisitRecord: (record: VisitRecord) => void;
  updateVisitRecord: (id: string, updates: Partial<VisitRecord>) => void;
  deleteVisitRecord: (id: string) => void;
  addTag: (tag: Tag) => void;
  filterRestaurants: () => void;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  register: (username: string, email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
  fetchData: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const useAppContext = () => {
  const context = useContext(AppContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAppContext must be used within an AppProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

interface AppProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

export const AppProvider: React.FC<AppProviderProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [restaurants, setRestaurants] = useState<Restaurant[]>([]);
  const [visitRecords, setVisitRecords] = useState<VisitRecord[]>([]);
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<Tag[]>([]);
  const [filteredRestaurants, setFilteredRestaurants] = useState<Restaurant[]>([]);
  const [filterOptions, setFilterOptions] = useState<FilterOptions>({});
  const [selectedRestaurant, setSelectedRestaurant] = useState<Restaurant | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Check if user is logged in on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = authService.getStoredUser();
    if (storedUser && authService.isAuthenticated()) {
      setUser(storedUser);
      fetchData();
    }
  }, []);

  // Fetch all data from backend
  const fetchData = async () => {
    if (!authService.isAuthenticated()) return;
    
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      // Fetch user profile
      const userProfile = await authService.getProfile();
      setUser(userProfile);

      // Fetch restaurants
      const restaurantsData = await restaurantService.getRestaurants();
      setRestaurants(restaurantsData);
      setFilteredRestaurants(restaurantsData);

      // Fetch visit records
      const visitsData = await visitService.getVisits();
      setVisitRecords(visitsData);

      // Fetch tags
      const tagsData = await tagService.getTags();
      setTags(tagsData);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error fetching data:', err);
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to fetch data');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const login = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await authService.login(email, password);
      setUser(response.user);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Login failed');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const register = async (username: string, email: string, password: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await authService.register(username, email, password);
      setUser(response.user);
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Registration failed');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    authService.logout();
    setUser(null);
    setRestaurants([]);
    setVisitRecords([]);
    setTags([]);
    setFilteredRestaurants([]);
  };

  const filterRestaurants = () => {
    let filtered = [...restaurants];

    // 搜尋篩選
    if (filterOptions.searchQuery) {
      const query = filterOptions.searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        restaurant.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.address.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        restaurant.tags.some(tag => tag.toLowerCase().includes(query))
      );
    }

    // 狀態篩選
    if (filterOptions.status && filterOptions.status.length > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        filterOptions.status!.includes(restaurant.status)
      );
    }

    // 標籤篩選
    if (filterOptions.tags && filterOptions.tags.length > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        filterOptions.tags!.some(tag => restaurant.tags.includes(tag))
      );
    }

    // 評分篩選
    if (filterOptions.minRating !== undefined) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        (restaurant.averageRating || 0) >= filterOptions.minRating!
      );
    }

    if (filterOptions.maxRating !== undefined) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(restaurant =>
        (restaurant.averageRating || 0) <= filterOptions.maxRating!
      );
    }

    setFilteredRestaurants(filtered);
  };

  const addRestaurant = async (restaurant: Restaurant) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const newRestaurant = await restaurantService.createRestaurant(restaurant);
      setRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      setFilteredRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      return newRestaurant;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const updateRestaurant = async (id: string, updates: Partial<Restaurant>) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await restaurantService.updateRestaurant(id, updates);
      setRestaurants(prev =>
        prev.map(restaurant =>
          restaurant.id === id ? { ...restaurant, ...updates } : restaurant
        )
      );
      filterRestaurants();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to update restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteRestaurant = async (id: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await restaurantService.deleteRestaurant(id);
      setRestaurants(prev => prev.filter(restaurant => restaurant.id !== id));
      setVisitRecords(prev => prev.filter(record => record.restaurantId !== id));
      filterRestaurants();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to delete restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const addVisitRecord = async (record: VisitRecord) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const newRecord = await visitService.createVisit(record);
      setVisitRecords(prev => [...prev, newRecord]);
      
      // Refresh restaurant data to update statistics
      await fetchData();
      
      return newRecord;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add visit record');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const updateVisitRecord = async (id: string, updates: Partial<VisitRecord>) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await visitService.updateVisit(id, updates);
      setVisitRecords(prev =>
        prev.map(record =>
          record.id === id ? { ...record, ...updates } : record
        )
      );
      
      // Refresh restaurant data to update statistics
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to update visit record');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteVisitRecord = async (id: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await visitService.deleteVisit(id);
      setVisitRecords(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== id));
      
      // Refresh restaurant data to update statistics
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to delete visit record');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const addTag = async (tag: Tag) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const newTag = await tagService.createTag(tag);
      setTags(prev => [...prev, newTag]);
      return newTag;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add tag');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // 當篩選選項改變時自動重新篩選
  useEffect(() => {
    filterRestaurants();
  }, [filterOptions, restaurants]);

  const value: AppContextType = {
    user,
    restaurants,
    visitRecords,
    tags,
    filteredRestaurants,
    filterOptions,
    selectedRestaurant,
    loading,
    error,
    setUser,
    setRestaurants,
    setVisitRecords,
    setTags,
    setFilterOptions,
    setSelectedRestaurant,
    addRestaurant,
    updateRestaurant,
    deleteRestaurant,
    addVisitRecord,
    updateVisitRecord,
    deleteVisitRecord,
    addTag,
    filterRestaurants,
    login,
    register,
    logout,
    fetchData,
  };

  return <AppContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AppContext.Provider>;
};

```

- Added login, register, logout

```typescriptreact
import { useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { logout } from '../services/auth.service';

interface LayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const Layout: React.FC<LayoutProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [anchorEl, setAnchorEl] = useState<null | HTMLElement>(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { user } = useAppContext();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const isMobile = useMediaQuery(theme.breakpoints.down('md'));

  const menuItems = [
    { text: '首頁', icon: <Home />, path: '/' },
    { text: '餐廳列表', icon: <Restaurant />, path: '/restaurants' },
    { text: '拜訪紀錄', icon: <ListIcon />, path: '/visits' },
    { text: '行事曆', icon: <CalendarToday />, path: '/calendar' },
    { text: '統計資料', icon: <BarChart />, path: '/stats' },
  ];

  const handleDrawerToggle = () => {
    setDrawerOpen(!drawerOpen);
  };

  const handleMenuOpen = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
    setAnchorEl(event.currentTarget);
  };

  const handleMenuClose = () => {
    setAnchorEl(null);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    handleMenuClose();
    navigate('/login');
  };

->

import { useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';

interface LayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

const Layout: React.FC<LayoutProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [anchorEl, setAnchorEl] = useState<null | HTMLElement>(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const { user, logout } = useAppContext();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const isMobile = useMediaQuery(theme.breakpoints.down('md'));

  const menuItems = [
    { text: '首頁', icon: <Home />, path: '/' },
    { text: '餐廳列表', icon: <Restaurant />, path: '/restaurants' },
    { text: '拜訪紀錄', icon: <ListIcon />, path: '/visits' },
    { text: '行事曆', icon: <CalendarToday />, path: '/calendar' },
    { text: '統計資料', icon: <BarChart />, path: '/stats' },
  ];

  const handleDrawerToggle = () => {
    setDrawerOpen(!drawerOpen);
  };

  const handleMenuOpen = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
    setAnchorEl(event.currentTarget);
  };

  const handleMenuClose = () => {
    setAnchorEl(null);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    handleMenuClose();
    navigate('/login');
  };

Providing a summary and instructions:


## Summary

The app is now using backend database data instead of mock data.

### Changes made

1. Updated `AppContext.tsx`:
   - Removed mock data
   - Made API calls async
   - Added `login`, `register`, `logout`
   - Added `loading` and `error` states
   - Fetch data on mount when authenticated
   - Refactor CRUD to use backend APIs

2. Updated `Layout.tsx`:
   - Use `logout` from context instead of the service

### How it works now

1. Authentication:
   - Checks auth on mount
   - If logged in, fetches data from the backend
   - Token stored in localStorage

2. Data flow:
   - `GET /api/restaurants`
   - `GET /api/visits`
   - `GET /api/tags`
   - All operations go through the backend API

3. CRUD operations:
   - Create: POST
   - Read: GET
   - Update: PUT
   - Delete: DELETE

### Next steps to test

1. Start the backend server:
   ```bash
   cd hw4/server
   npm run dev
   ```

2. Start the frontend:
   ```bash
   cd hw4/client
   npm run dev
   ```

3. Register a new user and test CRUD (create, read, update, delete).

### Features available

- User registration and login
- Create, update, delete restaurants
- Add visit records
- Add tags
- Filter and search restaurants
- Statistics from the backend
- Auto-refresh after changes

The app now reads and writes to your SQLite database.

```shellscript
   cd hw4/server
   npm run dev
```

```shellscript
   cd hw4/client
   npm run dev

```

撰寫流程說明文件。


```markdown
# API Key 設置與餐廳搜尋流程說明

## 1. Google Maps API Key 設定

### 需要的 API Key

後端需要設定以下環境變數：

#### 後端 API Key (`.env` 檔案)
```env
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的_Google_Maps_API_金鑰
```

#### 前端 API Key (`.env.local` 檔案)
```env
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的_Google_Maps_API_金鑰
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000
```

### 如何取得 Google Maps API Key

1. 前往 [Google Cloud Console](https://console.cloud.google.com/)
2. 建立新專案或選擇現有專案
3. 啟用以下 API：
   - **Geocoding API** - 地址轉換為經緯度
   - **Places API** - 搜尋餐廳
   - **Maps JavaScript API** - 前端地圖顯示

4. 建立 API Key：
   - 前往「憑證」頁面
   - 點擊「建立憑證」→「API 金鑰」
   - 複製金鑰並設置到環境變數中

### 必要性說明

- **後端** (`GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`)：
  - Geocoding API：建立餐廳時將地址轉換為座標
  - Places API：搜尋餐廳功能
  
- **前端** (`VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`)：
  - Maps JavaScript API：顯示地圖和標記

---

## 2. 餐廳搜尋與新增流程

### 流程圖

```
用戶操作 → 前端搜尋 → 後端 Places API → 顯示結果 → 選擇餐廳 → 儲存到資料庫
```

### 詳細流程說明

#### 步驟 1：搜尋餐廳

**前端呼叫：**
```typescript
// client/src/services/restaurant.service.ts
searchPlaces(query: string, location?: { lat: number; lng: number })
```

**後端處理：**
```typescript
// server/src/controllers/restaurant.controller.ts
POST /api/restaurants/search
{
  "query": "信義區餐廳",  // 搜尋關鍵字
  "lat": 25.0330,        // 可選：使用者當前位置
  "lng": 121.5654
}
```

**後端會：**
1. 呼叫 Google Places API 搜尋餐廳
2. 回傳餐廳列表（包含 place_id、名稱、地址、座標等）

#### 步驟 2：選擇並新增餐廳

**前端呼叫：**
```typescript
// 選擇餐廳後，建立新餐廳
addRestaurant({
  name: "一風堂拉麵",
  address: "台北市信義區松仁路100號",
  phone: "02-2345-6789",
  website: "https://ippudo.com.tw",
  description: "正統日式拉麵",
  tags: ["拉麵", "日式"],
  status: "want_to_visit"
})
```

**後端處理：**
```typescript
// server/src/controllers/restaurant.controller.ts
POST /api/restaurants
```

**後端會自動：**
1. 使用 Geocoding API 將地址轉換為座標
2. 儲存餐廳資料到資料庫
3. 自動建立標籤（如果不存在）
4. 回傳完整的餐廳物件

### 完整範例

#### 情境：搜尋並新增「信義區拉麵店」

```typescript
// 1. 搜尋餐廳
const places = await searchPlaces("信義區 拉麵");

// 結果範例：
[
  {
    placeId: "ChIJ...",
    name: "一風堂拉麵",
    address: "台北市信義區松仁路100號",
    lat: 25.0330,
    lng: 121.5654,
    phone: "02-2345-6789",
    rating: 4.5
  }
]

// 2. 選擇餐廳並新增
const newRestaurant = await addRestaurant({
  name: places[0].name,
  address: places[0].address,
  phone: places[0].phone,
  tags: ["拉麵", "日式", "午餐"],
  status: "want_to_visit"
});

// 3. 餐廳自動儲存到資料庫，並回傳完整資訊
// 包含自動產生的座標、ID 等
```

---

## 3. 環境變數設定

### 後端設定 (`hw4/server/.env`)

```env
# 資料庫
DB_PATH=./database/restaurants.db

# JWT
JWT_SECRET=your-secret-key-change-in-production

# Google Maps API
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIza... (你的 API Key)

# 伺服器
PORT=3000
NODE_ENV=development
```

### 前端設定 (`hw4/client/.env.local`)

```env
# API Base URL
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000

# Google Maps API Key
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIza... (你的 API Key)
```

---

## 4. API 端點總覽

### 餐廳相關

| 方法 | 端點 | 說明 |
|------|------|------|
| GET | `/api/restaurants` | 取得所有餐廳 |
| GET | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 取得單一餐廳 |
| POST | `/api/restaurants` | 新增餐廳（自動 geocode） |
| POST | `/api/restaurants/search` | 搜尋 Google Places |
| PUT | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 更新餐廳 |
| DELETE | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 刪除餐廳 |

### 認證相關

| 方法 | 端點 | 說明 |
|------|------|------|
| POST | `/api/auth/register` | 註冊 |
| POST | `/api/auth/login` | 登入 |
| GET | `/api/auth/profile` | 取得個人資料 |

---

## 5. 注意事項

### API Key 安全

1. **不要**將 API Key 提交到 Git
2. 使用 `.env` 檔案（已加入 `.gitignore`）
3. 在 Google Cloud Console 設定 API Key 限制：
   - HTTP 來源限制（前端）
   - IP 限制（後端）

### API 費用

- **Geocoding API**：每 1000 次請求 $5
- **Places API**：每次請求 $0.032
- **Maps JavaScript API**：每 1000 次載入 $7

建議：
- 在前幾千次請求使用免費額度
- 設定預算上限
- 監控 API 使用量

### 沒有 API Key 的情況

如果沒有設定 API Key：
- ✅ 用戶註冊/登入 **仍可正常運作**
- ✅ 手動輸入餐廳資訊 **仍可儲存**
- ❌ **無法**自動搜尋 Google Places 餐廳
- ❌ **無法**將地址轉換為座標
- ❌ **無法**顯示地圖

---

## 6. 測試流程

### 1. 啟動後端
```bash
cd hw4/server
npm run dev
```

### 2. 啟動前端
```bash
cd hw4/client
npm run dev
```

### 3. 測試步驟
1. 註冊新用戶
2. 登入
3. 嘗試搜尋餐廳
4. 選擇並新增餐廳到清單
5. 查看餐廳列表
6. 新增拜訪紀錄

---

## 7. 常見問題

### Q: 搜尋沒有結果？
A: 檢查後端 `.env` 的 `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` 是否正確

### Q: 地圖無法顯示？
A: 檢查前端 `.env.local` 的 `VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` 是否正確

### Q: API 呼叫次數過多？
A: 在 `GoogleMapsService` 中有 `apiCallCount` 監控，查看 console 輸出

### Q: 可以手動新增餐廳嗎？
A: 可以！不設定 API Key 也能手動輸入餐廳資訊並儲存

```

## 總結

### 1. **是否需要 API Key？**

需要兩個：
1. 後端：`GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`（用於 Geocoding API 與 Places API）
   - 地址轉經緯度
   - 搜尋餐廳
2. 前端：`VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`（用於 Maps JavaScript API）
   - 顯示地圖與標記

### 2. **餐廳搜尋與新增流程**

**完整流程：**
```
1. 搜尋階段：
   前端 → POST /api/restaurants/search
         ↓
   後端呼叫 Google Places API
         ↓
   回傳餐廳列表（place_id、名稱、地址、座標）

2. 新增階段：
   用戶選擇餐廳
         ↓
   前端 → POST /api/restaurants
         ↓
   後端自動 Geocode（地址轉座標）
         ↓
   儲存到資料庫
         ↓
   回傳完整餐廳資料
```

### 3. **已建立的檔案**

- `hw4/API_SETUP.md`：API Key 設定與流程說明文件

### 4. **沒有 API Key 也可以**

即使沒有設定 API Key，系統仍可：
- 用戶註冊/登入
- 手動輸入餐廳資訊
- 新增拜訪紀錄
- 查看統計

但無法使用：
- 自動搜尋 Google Places
- 將地址轉為座標
- 顯示地圖

### 5. **下次步驟**

1. 設定環境變數（如需使用 Google Maps 功能）
2. 啟動前後端
3. 測試完整流程

詳細說明請參考 `hw4/API_SETUP.md`。

```plaintext
1. 搜尋階段：
   前端 → POST /api/restaurants/search
         ↓
   後端呼叫 Google Places API
         ↓
   回傳餐廳列表（place_id、名稱、地址、座標）

2. 新增階段：
   用戶選擇餐廳
         ↓
   前端 → POST /api/restaurants
         ↓
   後端自動 Geocode（地址轉座標）
         ↓
   儲存到資料庫
         ↓
   回傳完整餐廳資料
```
