**User**

現在我無法加入新的餐廳，我想用關鍵字，例如鼎泰豐，搜尋餐廳並顯示結果，請幫我確認這部分的功能

---

**Cursor**

檢查餐廳搜尋與新增功能。先查看前端是否已實作搜尋 UI。


搜尋框只篩選既有餐廳，未實作新增餐廳流程。建立搜尋與新增頁面。


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Container,
  Box,
  TextField,
  Button,
  Typography,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Grid,
  Chip,
  CircularProgress,
  Alert,
  Rating,
  Paper,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemText,
  ListItemAvatar,
  Avatar,
  Divider,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  Search,
  Add,
  LocationOn,
  Phone,
  Language,
  Star,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { searchPlaces } from '../services/restaurant.service';

interface Place {
  placeId: string;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  phone?: string;
  website?: string;
  rating?: number;
  photoUrl?: string;
}

const RestaurantSearchPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { addRestaurant, loading, error } = useAppContext();
  
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<Place[]>([]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [addingRestaurant, setAddingRestaurant] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSearch = async () => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) {
      return;
    }

    setSearching(true);
    try {
      const places = await searchPlaces(searchQuery);
      setSearchResults(places);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Search error:', err);
      setSearchResults([]);
    } finally {
      setSearching(false);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      handleSearch();
    }
  };

  const handleAddRestaurant = async (place: Place) => {
    setAddingRestaurant(place.placeId);
    try {
      await addRestaurant({
        name: place.name,
        address: place.address,
        phone: place.phone,
        website: place.website,
        latitude: place.lat,
        longitude: place.lng,
        tags: [],
        status: 'want_to_visit' as any,
        averageRating: place.rating || 0,
        totalVisits: 0,
        totalSpent: 0,
        createdAt: new Date(),
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      });
      
      // 新增成功後導航回餐廳列表
      navigate('/restaurants');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Add restaurant error:', err);
    } finally {
      setAddingRestaurant(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      <Box sx={{ mb: 4 }}>
        <Typography variant="h4" component="h1" gutterBottom>
          搜尋餐廳
        </Typography>
        <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
          使用 Google Places API 搜尋餐廳並新增到您的收藏
        </Typography>
      </Box>

      {/* 搜尋欄 */}
      <Paper sx={{ p: 3, mb: 3 }}>
        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 2, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <TextField
            fullWidth
            placeholder="搜尋餐廳，例如：鼎泰豐、信義區餐廳..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            onKeyPress={handleKeyPress}
            InputProps={{
              startAdornment: <Search sx={{ mr: 1, color: 'text.secondary' }} />,
            }}
          />
          <Button
            variant="contained"
            onClick={handleSearch}
            disabled={searching || !searchQuery.trim()}
            startIcon={searching ? <CircularProgress size={20} /> : <Search />}
          >
            搜尋
          </Button>
        </Box>
      </Paper>

      {/* 錯誤訊息 */}
      {error && (
        <Alert severity="error" sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
          {error}
        </Alert>
      )}

      {/* 搜尋結果 */}
      {searchResults.length > 0 && (
        <Box>
          <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
            搜尋結果 ({searchResults.length})
          </Typography>
          <List>
            {searchResults.map((place, index) => (
              <React.Fragment key={place.placeId}>
                <ListItem
                  sx={{
                    border: '1px solid',
                    borderColor: 'divider',
                    borderRadius: 2,
                    mb: 2,
                    bgcolor: 'background.paper',
                  }}
                >
                  <ListItemAvatar>
                    <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                      <LocationOn />
                    </Avatar>
                  </ListItemAvatar>
                  <ListItemText
                    primary={
                      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                        <Typography variant="h6">{place.name}</Typography>
                        {place.rating && (
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 0.5 }}>
                            <Star sx={{ fontSize: 16, color: 'warning.main' }} />
                            <Typography variant="body2">
                              {place.rating.toFixed(1)}
                            </Typography>
                          </Box>
                        )}
                      </Box>
                    }
                    secondary={
                      <Box>
                        <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mb: 1 }}>
                          <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'middle' }} />
                          {place.address}
                        </Typography>
                        {place.phone && (
                          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mb: 0.5 }}>
                            <Phone sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'middle' }} />
                            {place.phone}
                          </Typography>
                        )}
                        {place.website && (
                          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                            <Language sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'middle' }} />
                            {place.website}
                          </Typography>
                        )}
                      </Box>
                    }
                  />
                  <Button
                    variant="contained"
                    startIcon={addingRestaurant === place.placeId ? <CircularProgress size={16} /> : <Add />}
                    onClick={() => handleAddRestaurant(place)}
                    disabled={addingRestaurant === place.placeId || loading}
                  >
                    新增
                  </Button>
                </ListItem>
              </React.Fragment>
            ))}
          </List>
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 空狀態 */}
      {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && searchQuery && (
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            沒有找到符合的餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mt: 1 }}>
            請嘗試使用不同的關鍵字搜尋
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      )}

      {/* 提示訊息 */}
      {!searching && searchResults.length === 0 && !searchQuery && (
        <Paper sx={{ p: 4, textAlign: 'center', bgcolor: 'action.hover' }}>
          <Search sx={{ fontSize: 64, color: 'text.secondary', mb: 2 }} />
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
            開始搜尋餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
            輸入餐廳名稱或地點來搜尋，例如：鼎泰豐、信義區火鍋、台北拉麵...
          </Typography>
        </Paper>
      )}
    </Container>
  );
};

export default RestaurantSearchPage;

```

  const addRestaurant = async (restaurant: Restaurant) => {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const newRestaurant = await restaurantService.createRestaurant(restaurant);
      setRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      setFilteredRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      return newRestaurant;

      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const addRestaurant = async (restaurant: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<Restaurant> => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const newRestaurant = await restaurantService.createRestaurant(restaurant);
      setRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      setFilteredRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      return newRestaurant;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

                  <Route path="/restaurants/:id" element={<ProtectedRoute><RestaurantDetailPage /></ProtectedRoute>} />
```

```markdown
# 餐廳搜尋功能使用指南

## 功能說明

新增了餐廳搜尋功能，讓您可以透過 Google Places API 搜尋餐廳並快速新增到您的收藏列表中。

## 如何使用

### 1. 進入搜尋頁面

有兩種方式進入搜尋頁面：

#### 方式一：從餐廳列表頁
1. 點擊左側選單的「餐廳列表」
2. 點擊右上角的「搜尋餐廳」按鈕

#### 方式二：直接進入
在網址列輸入：`http://localhost:5173/restaurants/search`

### 2. 搜尋餐廳

1. 在搜尋框中輸入關鍵字
   - 餐廳名稱：例如「鼎泰豐」、「麥當勞」
   - 區域餐廳：例如「信義區餐廳」、「台北火鍋」
   - 類型餐廳：例如「拉麵」、「義大利料理」

2. 點擊「搜尋」按鈕或按下 Enter 鍵

3. 系統會顯示搜尋結果，包含：
   - 餐廳名稱
   - 地址
   - 電話（如果有）
   - 網站（如果有）
   - Google 評分

### 3. 新增餐廳

1. 在搜尋結果中找到想新增的餐廳
2. 點擊餐廳卡片的「新增」按鈕
3. 系統會自動將餐廳新增到您的收藏
4. 自動跳轉回餐廳列表頁面

## 功能特點

### ✅ 即時搜尋
- 使用 Google Places API 即時搜尋
- 結果包含完整的餐廳資訊
- 自動取得餐廳的經緯度座標

### ✅ 自動整合
- 新增的餐廳會自動出現在餐廳列表中
- 可以在地圖檢視中看到新增的餐廳
- 支援後續的編輯和標籤管理

### ✅ 錯誤處理
- 如果 API Key 未設定，會顯示錯誤訊息
- 搜尋無結果時提供友善提示
- 網路錯誤會有適當的錯誤處理

## 注意事項

### 需要 API Key

此功能需要設定 Google Maps API Key：

1. **後端設定** (`hw4/server/.env`)：
   ```env
   GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的_API_Key
   ```

2. **前端設定** (`hw4/client/.env.local`)：
   ```env
   VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=你的_API_Key
   ```

詳細設定請參考 `API_SETUP.md`

### 搜尋限制

- 需要啟用 Google Places API
- 有 API 呼叫次數限制和費用
- 建議設定預算上限避免超支

### 沒有 API Key 的情況

如果沒有設定 API Key：
- ❌ 無法使用搜尋功能
- ✅ 仍可手動新增餐廳
- ✅ 其他功能正常運作

## 技術細節

### 前端流程

```
用戶輸入 → 呼叫 searchPlaces API → 顯示結果 → 點擊新增 → 呼叫 createRestaurant API → 跳轉回列表
```

### API 端點

#### 搜尋餐廳
```
POST /api/restaurants/search
Body: { query: "搜尋關鍵字" }
Query: { lat?: number, lng?: number }  // 可選：使用者位置
```

#### 新增餐廳
```
POST /api/restaurants
Body: { name, address, phone, website, latitude, longitude, tags, status }
```

### 資料流程

1. **搜尋階段**：
   - 前端呼叫 `searchPlaces(query)`
   - 後端使用 Google Places API 搜尋
   - 回傳結果列表

2. **新增階段**：
   - 前端選擇餐廳並呼叫 `addRestaurant()`
   - 後端自動 Geocode（如果沒有座標）
   - 儲存到資料庫
   - 回傳完整餐廳物件

## 範例

### 搜尋「鼎泰豐」

```
1. 輸入「鼎泰豐」並搜尋
2. 顯示結果：
   - 鼎泰豐（信義店）
   - 鼎泰豐（101店）
   - ...
3. 選擇想新增的分店
4. 點擊「新增」
5. 完成！餐廳已加入收藏
```

### 搜尋「信義區火鍋」

```
1. 輸入「信義區火鍋」並搜尋
2. 顯示該區域的相關餐廳
3. 逐一新增需要的餐廳
4. 可在餐廳列表查看所有新增的餐廳
```

## 疑難排解

### Q: 搜尋沒有結果？
A: 檢查：
1. API Key 是否正確設定
2. Google Places API 是否已啟用
3. 網路連線是否正常
4. API 配額是否用完

### Q: 無法新增餐廳？
A: 檢查：
1. 是否已登入
2. 後端是否正常運作
3. 查看瀏覽器 Console 的錯誤訊息

### Q: 搜尋結果不準確？
A: 嘗試：
1. 使用更具體的關鍵字
2. 加入地點資訊（例如「台北鼎泰豐」）
3. 使用英文關鍵字

## 相關檔案

- **前端頁面**：`hw4/client/src/pages/RestaurantSearchPage.tsx`
- **服務函數**：`hw4/client/src/services/restaurant.service.ts`
- **後端控制器**：`hw4/server/src/controllers/restaurant.controller.ts`
- **API 服務**：`hw4/server/src/services/google-maps.service.ts`

```

4. 查看結果列表
5. 點擊「新增」加入收藏

- 前端 `.env.local`：`VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`

調整搜尋參數，移除 `type: 'restaurant'` 以避免過濾餐廳；修正條件以正確傳遞 `location` 與 `radius`。


```typescript
  static async searchPlaces(query: string, location?: LatLng): Promise<Place[]> {
    if (!API_KEY) {
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not configured');
      return [];
    }

    try {
      this.apiCallCount++;
      const params: any = {
        query: `餐廳 ${query}`,
        key: API_KEY,
        language: 'zh-TW',
        type: 'restaurant',
      };

      // 如果有指定位置，加入 locationbias
      if (location) {
        params.location = `${location.lat},${location.lng}`;
        params.radius = 10000; // 10km
      }

      const response = await axios.get('https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/textsearch/json', {
        params,
      });

->

  static async searchPlaces(query: string, location?: LatLng): Promise<Place[]> {
    if (!API_KEY) {
      console.warn('Google Maps API key not configured');
      return [];
    }

    try {
      this.apiCallCount++;
      const params: any = {
        query: query,  // 直接使用查詢字串，不強制加上「餐廳」
        key: API_KEY,
        language: 'zh-TW',
      };

      // 如果有指定位置，使用 near-by search 或 location bias
      if (location) {
        params.location = `${location.lat},${location.lng}`;
        params.radius = 10000; // 10km
      }

      console.log('Searching with params:', params);

      const response = await axios.get('https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/place/textsearch/json', {
        params,
      });
```

檢查前端如何呼叫 API，確保參數正確傳遞：


`searchPlaces` 的 `params` 傳遞不正確。修正為將 location 放在 URL query 參數。


```typescript
export const searchPlaces = async (query: string, location?: { lat: number; lng: number }): Promise<any[]> => {
  const params = location ? { lat: location.lat, lng: location.lng } : {};
  const response = await api.post('/api/restaurants/search', { query }, { params });
  return response.data.data;
};

->

export const searchPlaces = async (query: string, location?: { lat: number; lng: number }): Promise<any[]> => {
  const queryParams = location ? { lat: location.lat.toString(), lng: location.lng.toString() } : {};
  const response = await api.post('/api/restaurants/search', { query }, { params: queryParams });
  return response.data.data;
};
```

## 🐛 常見問題排查

### 問題 1：搜尋沒有結果

**可能原因：**
1. API Key 未正確設定
2. API Key 已失效或配額用完
3. 網路連線問題
4. Google Places API 未啟用

**解決方案：**
1. 檢查 `.env` 檔案是否存在且內容正確
2. 重啟後端服務器
3. 檢查 Google Cloud Console 中的 API 狀態
4. 查看後端 Console 的錯誤訊息

### 問題 2：API Key not configured

**錯誤訊息：**
```
Google Maps API key not configured
```

**解決方案：**
1. 確認 `.env` 檔案在 `hw4/server/` 目錄下
2. 確認變數名稱是 `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY`（全大寫）
3. 重新啟動後端服務器

### 問題 3：CORS 錯誤

**錯誤訊息：**
```
Access to XMLHttpRequest blocked by CORS policy
```

**解決方案：**
- 已在 `app.ts` 中設定 `app.use(cors())`
- 確認後端已正確啟動

### 問題 4：401 Unauthorized

**錯誤訊息：**
```
401 Access denied. No token provided.
```

**解決方案：**
1. 確認已登入系統
2. 檢查 localStorage 中是否有 JWT token
3. 重新登入以獲取新 token

# API Key 問題排查指南

## 問題：後端顯示 "Google Maps API key not configured"

### 已完成的修復

1. ✅ 已將 `dotenv.config()` 移到 `app.ts` 的最開頭
2. ✅ 已新增 debug logging 到 `google-maps.service.ts`
3. ✅ 已新增 `/api/debug/env` 端點來檢查環境變數

### 測試步驟

#### 1. 重新啟動後端

```bash
cd hw4/server
npm run dev
```

#### 2. 檢查環境變數

訪問：`http://localhost:3000/api/debug/env`

**預期結果：**
```json
{
  "GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY": "Set",
  "API_KEY_LENGTH": 39,
  "PORT": "3000"
}
```

#### 3. 檢查後端 Console

查看是否有這行：
```
Google Maps API Key loaded: Yes (AIzaSyB_cU...)
```

### 可能的原因與解決方案

#### 原因 1：`.env` 檔案位置錯誤

**檢查：**
```bash
cd hw4/server
ls -la | grep .env
```

**應該看到：**
```
-rw-r--r--   1 user  ...   180 .env
```

#### 原因 2：`.env` 檔案格式錯誤

**檢查：**
```bash
cd hw4/server
cat .env
```

**應該看到：**
```
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIzaSyB_cUhvh7BWPno0IAeLULnZUrgm2Vev45Q
PORT=3000
NODE_ENV=development
JWT_SECRET=your-secret-key-change-in-production
```

**注意事項：**
- 每行一個變數
- 不要有多餘的空格
- 值不需要引號

#### 原因 3：後端未正確重新啟動

**解決方案：**
1. 完全停止後端（Ctrl+C）
2. 重新啟動：
   ```bash
   npm run dev
   ```

#### 原因 4：環境變數被其他程序覆蓋

**檢查 PowerShell 環境變數：**
```powershell
$env:GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY
```

**如果有設定，清除它：**
```powershell
Remove-Item Env:\GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY
```

### 快速測試

#### 方法 1：直接在程式碼中測試

在 `server.ts` 中加入：
```typescript
console.log('API Key on startup:', process.env.GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY);
```

#### 方法 2：使用 curl 測試

```bash
curl http://localhost:3000/api/debug/env
```

#### 方法 3：在瀏覽器中測試

訪問：`http://localhost:3000/api/debug/env`

### 如果問題仍然存在

#### 臨時解決方案：直接在程式碼中設定

在 `hw4/server/src/services/google-maps.service.ts` 中：

```typescript
const API_KEY = process.env.GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY || 'AIzaSyB_cUhvh7BWPno0IAeLULnZUrgm2Vev45Q';
```

⚠️ **警告**：這只是臨時方案，不應該提交到 Git！

#### 永久解決方案：檢查檔案編碼

`.env` 檔案應該使用 UTF-8 編碼：

```powershell
# 檢查編碼
[System.IO.File]::ReadAllText(".env", [System.Text.Encoding]::Default)

# 重新建立檔案（UTF-8）
@"
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIzaSyB_cUhvh7BWPno0IAeLULnZUrgm2Vev45Q
PORT=3000
NODE_ENV=development
JWT_SECRET=your-secret-key-change-in-production
"@ | Out-File -FilePath .env -Encoding utf8
```

### 驗證修復

1. 重新啟動後端
2. 訪問 `http://localhost:3000/api/debug/env`
3. 確認 `GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY` 顯示為 "Set"

```

    setError(null);
    try {
      const newRestaurant = await restaurantService.createRestaurant(restaurant);
      setRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      setFilteredRestaurants(prev => [...prev, newRestaurant]);
      return newRestaurant;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

->

  const addRestaurant = async (restaurant: Partial<Restaurant>): Promise<Restaurant> => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const newRestaurant = await restaurantService.createRestaurant(restaurant);
      const updatedRestaurants = [...restaurants, newRestaurant];
      setRestaurants(updatedRestaurants);
      
      // Re-apply filters to include the new restaurant if it matches
      setFilterOptions(prevOptions => {
        filterRestaurants();
        return prevOptions;
      });
      
      return newRestaurant;
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to add restaurant');
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };
```

在 `RestaurantSearchPage` 中調整導航時機，確保新餐廳已建立，並在找不到餐廳時加入載入狀態：


```typescriptreact

  const handleAddRestaurant = async (place: Place) => {
    setAddingRestaurant(place.placeId);
    try {
      // Wait for the restaurant to be added and data to be refreshed
      await addRestaurant({
        name: place.name,
        address: place.address,
        phone: place.phone,
        website: place.website,
        latitude: place.lat,
        longitude: place.lng,
        tags: [],
        status: 'want_to_visit',
      });
      
      // Wait a bit to ensure state is updated
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      
      // Navigate to restaurant list
      navigate('/restaurants');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Add restaurant error:', err);
    } finally {
      setAddingRestaurant(null);
    }
  };
```

在 `RestaurantDetailPage` 區分三種狀態：

```markdown
# 美食探索 - 餐廳收藏及探索系統

## 📖 專案簡介

這是一個基於 **React + TypeScript + Material UI + Express + SQLite** 的餐廳收藏及探索系統，讓使用者可以：

- 🔍 使用 Google Places API 搜尋並收藏餐廳
- 📍 在地圖上視覺化餐廳位置
- 📝 記錄拜訪紀錄、評分與花費
- 📊 查看統計分析與趨勢
- 🏷️ 使用標籤分類管理餐廳

### 使用情境

1. **日常用餐規劃**：收藏想去的餐廳，記錄拜訪後的心得
2. **用餐習慣追蹤**：統計每月花費、最常去的餐廳類型
3. **社交分享**：查看評分最高或最常拜訪的餐廳
4. **地理探索**：在地圖上探索附近或特定區域的餐廳

---

## 🚀 快速開始

### 前置需求

- Node.js 18+ 
- npm 或 yarn
- Google Maps API Key（用於地圖與搜尋功能）

### 1. 安裝依賴

```bash
# 安裝前端依賴
cd client
npm install

# 安裝後端依賴
cd ../server
npm install
```

### 2. 設定環境變數

#### 後端環境變數 (`hw4/server/.env`)

建立 `.env` 檔案：

```env
# 伺服器設定
PORT=3000
NODE_ENV=development

# JWT 認證
JWT_SECRET=your-secret-key-change-in-production

# Google Maps API（必要）
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=your-google-maps-api-key

# 資料庫
DB_PATH=./database/restaurants.db
```

#### 前端環境變數 (`hw4/client/.env.local`)

建立 `.env.local` 檔案：

```env
# API Base URL
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000

# Google Maps API Key（必要）
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=your-google-maps-api-key
```

### 3. 啟動服務

#### 啟動後端

```bash
cd server
npm run dev
```

後端將在 http://localhost:3000 運行

#### 啟動前端

```bash
cd client
npm run dev
```

前端將在 http://localhost:5173 運行

### 4. 開始使用

1. 打開瀏覽器訪問 http://localhost:5173
2. 註冊新帳號或登入
3. 開始搜尋並收藏餐廳！

---

## 🏗️ 系統架構

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         前端 (React)                         │
│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐      │
│  │   React App  │  │ Context API  │  │  Material UI │      │
│  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘      │
│         │                  │                  │              │
│         └──────────────────┼──────────────────┘              │
│                            │                                 │
│                     ┌──────▼──────┐                          │
│                     │ Axios Client│                          │
│                     └──────┬──────┘                          │
└────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┘
                             │ HTTP/REST
┌────────────────────────────▼─────────────────────────────────┐
│                     後端 (Express)                           │
│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐      │
│  │   Routes     │→ │ Controllers  │→ │   Services   │      │
│  └──────────────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘      │
│                            │                  │              │
│                     ┌──────▼──────┐   ┌──────▼──────────┐   │
│                     │ Middleware  │   │ Google Maps API │   │
│                     │   (JWT)     │   │   (Places/      │   │
│                     └─────────────┘   │   Geocoding)    │   │
│                                       └─────────────────┘   │
│                            │                                 │
│                     ┌──────▼──────┐                          │
│                     │   SQLite    │                          │
│                     │  Database   │                          │
│                     └─────────────┘                          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 技術棧

#### 前端
- **React 18** - UI 框架
- **TypeScript** - 型別安全
- **Vite** - 建置工具
- **Material UI** - UI 元件庫
- **React Router** - 路由
- **Axios** - HTTP 客戶端
- **Google Maps JavaScript API** - 地圖功能

#### 後端
- **Node.js** - 執行環境
- **Express** - Web 框架
- **TypeScript** - 型別安全
- **SQLite** - 資料庫（better-sqlite3）
- **JWT** - 認證（jsonwebtoken）
- **bcrypt** - 密碼雜湊
- **dotenv** - 環境變數
- **Google Maps Services** - Places API, Geocoding API

---

## 📝 API 文件

### 認證相關

#### 1. 使用者註冊

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "username": "testuser",
    "email": "test@example.com",
    "password": "password123"
  }'
```

**回應：**
```json
{
  "message": "User registered successfully",
  "token": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIs...",
  "user": {
    "id": "1",
    "username": "testuser",
    "email": "test@example.com"
  }
}
```

#### 2. 使用者登入

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/login \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "email": "test@example.com",
    "password": "password123"
  }'
```

**回應：**
```json
{
  "message": "Login successful",
  "token": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIs...",
  "user": {
    "id": "1",
    "username": "testuser",
    "email": "test@example.com"
  }
}
```

#### 3. 取得個人資料（需要認證）

```bash
curl -X GET http://localhost:3000/api/auth/profile \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIs..."
```

**回應：**
```json
{
  "id": "1",
  "username": "testuser",
  "email": "test@example.com"
}
```

### 餐廳相關

#### 4. 搜尋餐廳（Google Places）

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/restaurants/search \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIs..." \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "query": "信義區餐廳"
  }'
```

**回應：**
```json
{
  "message": "Places found",
  "data": [
    {
      "placeId": "ChIJ...",
      "name": "一風堂拉麵",
      "address": "台北市信義區松仁路100號",
      "lat": 25.0330,
      "lng": 121.5654,
      "phone": "02-2345-6789",
      "rating": 4.5
    }
  ]
}
```

#### 5. 新增餐廳

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/restaurants \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIs..." \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "name": "一風堂拉麵",
    "address": "台北市信義區松仁路100號",
    "phone": "02-2345-6789",
    "website": "https://ippudo.com.tw",
    "description": "正統日式拉麵",
    "tags": ["拉麵", "日式"],
    "status": "want_to_visit"
  }'
```

**回應：**
```json
{
  "id": "1",
  "name": "一風堂拉麵",
  "address": "台北市信義區松仁路100號",
  "latitude": 25.0330,
  "longitude": 121.5654,
  "status": "want_to_visit",
  "tags": ["拉麵", "日式"],
  ...
}
```

### 完整授權流程範例

```bash
# 1. 註冊
TOKEN=$(curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/register \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "username": "demo",
    "email": "demo@example.com",
    "password": "demo123"
  }' | jq -r '.token')

echo "Token: $TOKEN"

# 2. 使用 Token 搜尋餐廳
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/restaurants/search \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "query": "鼎泰豐"
  }'

# 3. 新增餐廳
curl -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/restaurants \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "name": "鼎泰豐",
    "address": "台北市大安區信義路二段194號",
    "tags": ["小籠包", "中式"],
    "status": "want_to_visit"
  }'

# 4. 取得所有餐廳
curl -X GET http://localhost:3000/api/restaurants \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN"
```

### API 端點總覽

| 方法 | 端點 | 說明 | 認證 |
|------|------|------|------|
| POST | `/api/auth/register` | 註冊 | ❌ |
| POST | `/api/auth/login` | 登入 | ❌ |
| GET | `/api/auth/profile` | 個人資料 | ✅ |
| GET | `/api/restaurants` | 取得所有餐廳 | ✅ |
| GET | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 取得單一餐廳 | ✅ |
| POST | `/api/restaurants` | 新增餐廳 | ✅ |
| POST | `/api/restaurants/search` | 搜尋 Google Places | ✅ |
| PUT | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 更新餐廳 | ✅ |
| DELETE | `/api/restaurants/:id` | 刪除餐廳 | ✅ |
| GET | `/api/visits` | 取得所有拜訪紀錄 | ✅ |
| POST | `/api/visits` | 新增拜訪紀錄 | ✅ |
| GET | `/api/tags` | 取得所有標籤 | ✅ |

## 🔧 環境變數範例

### `.env.example` (後端)

```env
# 伺服器設定
PORT=3000
NODE_ENV=development

# JWT 認證（請在正式環境使用更複雜的密鑰）
JWT_SECRET=your-secret-key-change-in-production

# Google Maps API Key
# 取得方式：https://console.cloud.google.com/
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIzaSy...

# 資料庫路徑
DB_PATH=./database/restaurants.db
```

### `.env.local.example` (前端)

```env
# API 伺服器 URL
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000

# Google Maps API Key
VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIzaSy...
```

---

## 🎯 功能特色

### 📍 地圖功能
- Google Maps 整合顯示餐廳位置
- 不同顏色標記不同狀態（想去/去過/常去）
- 地圖與列表檢視切換

### 🍽️ 餐廳管理
- Google Places API 搜尋餐廳
- 自動將地址轉換為座標
- 三種狀態分類（想去/去過/常去）
- 自訂標籤系統
- 完整餐廳資訊（地址、電話、網站、描述）

### 📝 拜訪紀錄
- 記錄拜訪時間、評分、備註
- 花費記錄與統計
- 照片上傳（預留功能）

### 📊 統計分析
- 綜合統計（餐廳數、拜訪次數、總花費、平均評分）
- 排行榜（最常拜訪、評分最高、花費最多）
- 標籤分析
- 月度趨勢圖

### 📅 行事曆檢視
- 月曆形式顯示拜訪紀錄
- 點擊日期查看詳情
- 視覺化標記

### 🔍 搜尋與篩選
- 關鍵字搜尋
- 狀態/標籤/評分篩選
- 即時搜尋結果

---

## ⚠️ 已知問題

1. **Google Maps API 載入**：首次載入地圖可能需要等待 API 載入完成
2. **圖片上傳**：拜訪照片上傳功能尚未實作
3. **離線支援**：目前不支援離線模式
4. **狀態同步**：新增餐廳後需要小延遲以確保狀態同步

## 🔒 安全性風險說明

### Google Maps API Key 設定

⚠️ **重要**：如果使用 **Server Key** 且無 IP 限制，請注意以下風險：

#### 風險
1. **未授權存取**：任何人取得 API Key 都可能無限使用您的配額
2. **費用風險**：惡意使用者可能導致高額費用
3. **服務濫用**：過度使用可能導致 API 限流或暫停

#### 建議措施
1. **限制 HTTP 來源**（前端 Key）：
   - 在 Google Cloud Console 設定應用程式網域
   - 例如：`http://localhost:5173`, `https://yourdomain.com`

2. **限制 IP**（後端 Key）：
   - 僅允許您的伺服器 IP
   - 避免設為 0.0.0.0/0（允許所有 IP）

3. **設定配額限制**：
   - 在 Google Cloud Console 設定每日配額上限
   - 啟用計費告警

4. **定期輪換 Key**：
   - 定期更新 API Key
   - 移除不再使用的 Key

5. **不要提交到 Git**：
   - 確保 `.env` 在 `.gitignore` 中
   - 使用環境變數而非硬編碼

### 最佳實踐範例

```env
# ✅ 正確：使用環境變數
GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY=AIzaSy...

# ❌ 錯誤：硬編碼在程式碼中
const API_KEY = "AIzaSy..."; // 不安全！
```

hw4/
├── client/                    # 前端應用
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── components/        # UI 元件
│   │   ├── pages/            # 頁面元件
│   │   ├── context/          # Context API
│   │   ├── services/         # API 服務
│   │   └── types/            # TypeScript 型別
│   ├── .env.local           # 前端環境變數
│   └── package.json
│
├── server/                   # 後端應用
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── controllers/      # 控制器
│   │   ├── middleware/       # 中介軟體
│   │   ├── routes/          # 路由
│   │   ├── services/        # 業務邏輯
│   │   └── utils/           # 工具函數
│   ├── database/            # SQLite 資料庫
│   ├── .env                 # 後端環境變數
│   └── package.json
│
└── README.md                # 專案說明文件
```

**Cursor**

檢視拜訪相關程式碼，定位新增拜訪無法加入紀錄的原因。


繼續檢視相關函數：


檢查 visit service：


檢查後端的 visits controller：


檢查 RestaurantDetailPage 新增拜訪的實作：


檢視 RestaurantDetailPage.tsx：


問題在 `AddVisitForm`（約第 395 行）：傳送了包含 `id`, `userId` 等額外欄位，而後端只需部分欄位。檢查 `createVisit` 預期的欄位結構：


修正 `handleSubmit`，只傳送後端預期的欄位：


```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Container,
  Grid,
  Card,
  CardContent,
  Typography,
  Box,
  Chip,
  Button,
  Rating,
  Avatar,
  IconButton,
  Dialog,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogContent,
  DialogActions,
  TextField,
  List,
  ListItem,
  ListItemText,
  ListItemAvatar,
} from '@mui/material';
import {
  ArrowBack,
  LocationOn,
  Phone,
  Language,
  Edit,
  Add,
  Delete,
  CalendarToday,
  AttachMoney,
} from '@mui/icons-material';
import { useAppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
import { RestaurantStatus } from '../types';
// import type { VisitRecord } from '../types';
import GoogleMap from '../components/GoogleMap';

const RestaurantDetailPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { 
    restaurants, 
    visitRecords, 
    deleteVisitRecord 
  } = useAppContext();

  const [showAddVisitDialog, setShowAddVisitDialog] = useState(false);
  // const [showEditDialog, setShowEditDialog] = useState(false);
  const [deleteDialogOpen, setDeleteDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [visitToDelete, setVisitToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const restaurant = restaurants.find(r => r.id === id);
  
  if (!restaurant && restaurants.length > 0) {
    return (
      <Container maxWidth="lg">
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="h6" color="text.secondary">
            找不到指定的餐廳
          </Typography>
          <Button
            variant="contained"
            onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants')}
            sx={{ mt: 2 }}
          >
            返回餐廳列表
          </Button>
        </Box>
      </Container>
    );
  }

  // Show loading state if restaurants are still being loaded
  if (!restaurant && restaurants.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Container maxWidth="lg">
        <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 8 }}>
          <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
            載入中...
          </Typography>
        </Box>
      </Container>
    );
  }

  // This should not happen, but handle it gracefully
  if (!restaurant) {
    return null;
  }

  const restaurantVisits = visitRecords
    .filter(record => record.restaurantId === restaurant.id)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.visitDate).getTime() - new Date(a.visitDate).getTime());

  const getStatusLabel = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return '想去';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return '去過';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return '常去';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: RestaurantStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case RestaurantStatus.WANT_TO_VISIT:
        return 'default';
      case RestaurantStatus.VISITED:
        return 'primary';
      case RestaurantStatus.FREQUENT:
        return 'secondary';
      default:
        return 'default';
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteVisit = (visitId: string) => {
    setVisitToDelete(visitId);
    setDeleteDialogOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
    if (visitToDelete) {
      deleteVisitRecord(visitToDelete);
      setDeleteDialogOpen(false);
      setVisitToDelete(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Container maxWidth="lg">
      {/* 返回按鈕 */}
      <Box sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
        <Button
          startIcon={<ArrowBack />}
          onClick={() => navigate('/restaurants')}
        >
          返回餐廳列表
        </Button>
      </Box>

      <Grid container spacing={3}>
        {/* 餐廳基本資訊 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={8}>
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', mb: 2 }}>
                <Typography variant="h4" component="h1">
                  {restaurant.name}
                </Typography>
                <Box>
                  <Chip 
                    label={getStatusLabel(restaurant.status)} 
                    color={getStatusColor(restaurant.status) as any}
                    sx={{ mr: 1 }}
                  />
                  <IconButton onClick={() => {/* setShowEditDialog(true) */}}>
                    <Edit />
                  </IconButton>
                </Box>
              </Box>

              <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                <LocationOn sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                {restaurant.address}
              </Typography>

              {restaurant.phone && (
                <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <Phone sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  {restaurant.phone}
                </Typography>
              )}

              {restaurant.website && (
                <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary" gutterBottom>
                  <Language sx={{ fontSize: 20, mr: 1, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                  <a href={restaurant.website} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
                    {restaurant.website}
                  </a>
                </Typography>
              )}

              {restaurant.description && (
                <Typography variant="body1" sx={{ mt: 2 }}>
                  {restaurant.description}
                </Typography>
              )}

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 1, mt: 2 }}>
                {(restaurant.tags || []).map((tag) => (
                  <Chip key={tag} label={tag} variant="outlined" />
                ))}
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* 地圖 */}
          <Card sx={{ mt: 3 }}>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                位置
              </Typography>
              <GoogleMap 
                restaurants={[restaurant]} 
                center={{ lat: restaurant.latitude, lng: restaurant.longitude }}
                zoom={15}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>

        {/* 統計資訊和拜訪紀錄 */}
        <Grid item xs={12} md={4}>
          {/* 統計資訊 */}
          <Card sx={{ mb: 3 }}>
            <CardContent>
              <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
                統計資訊
              </Typography>
              
              {restaurant.averageRating && (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
                  <Rating value={restaurant.averageRating} readOnly />
                  <Typography variant="h6" sx={{ ml: 1 }}>
                    {restaurant.averageRating.toFixed(1)}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              )}

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總拜訪次數:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  {(restaurant.totalVisits || 0)}
                </Typography>
              </Box>

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  總花費:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  NT$ {(restaurant.totalSpent || 0).toLocaleString()}
                </Typography>
              </Box>

              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', mb: 1 }}>
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                  平均花費:
                </Typography>
                <Typography variant="body2">
                  NT$ {(restaurant.totalVisits || 0) > 0 ? Math.round((restaurant.totalSpent || 0) / (restaurant.totalVisits || 1)).toLocaleString() : 0}
                </Typography>
              </Box>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* 拜訪紀錄 */}
          <Card>
            <CardContent>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', mb: 2 }}>
                <Typography variant="h6">
                  拜訪紀錄
                </Typography>
                <Button
                  variant="contained"
                  size="small"
                  startIcon={<Add />}
                  onClick={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(true)}
                >
                  新增拜訪
                </Button>
              </Box>

              {restaurantVisits.length > 0 ? (
                <List>
                  {restaurantVisits.map((visit) => (
                    <ListItem key={visit.id} divider>
                      <ListItemAvatar>
                        <Avatar sx={{ bgcolor: 'primary.main' }}>
                          <CalendarToday />
                        </Avatar>
                      </ListItemAvatar>
                      <ListItemText
                        primary={
                          <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 1 }}>
                            <Typography variant="body2">
                              {new Date(visit.visitDate).toLocaleDateString('zh-TW')}
                            </Typography>
                            <Rating value={visit.rating} readOnly size="small" />
                          </Box>
                        }
                        secondary={
                          <Box>
                            <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                              <AttachMoney sx={{ fontSize: 14, mr: 0.5, verticalAlign: 'text-bottom' }} />
                              NT$ {visit.totalSpent}
                            </Typography>
                            {visit.comment && (
                              <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                                {visit.comment}
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                          </Box>
                        }
                      />
                      <IconButton
                        size="small"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteVisit(visit.id)}
                        color="error"
                      >
                        <Delete />
                      </IconButton>
                    </ListItem>
                  ))}
                </List>
              ) : (
                <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ textAlign: 'center', py: 2 }}>
                  還沒有拜訪紀錄
                </Typography>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </Grid>
      </Grid>

      {/* 新增拜訪對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={showAddVisitDialog} onClose={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)} maxWidth="sm" fullWidth>
        <DialogTitle>新增拜訪紀錄</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
            為 {restaurant.name} 新增拜訪紀錄
          </Typography>
          <AddVisitForm 
            restaurant={restaurant} 
            onClose={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)}
            onSave={() => setShowAddVisitDialog(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* 刪除確認對話框 */}
      <Dialog open={deleteDialogOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>
        <DialogTitle>確認刪除</DialogTitle>
        <DialogContent>
          <Typography>
            確定要刪除這個拜訪紀錄嗎？此操作無法復原。
          </Typography>
        </DialogContent>
        <DialogActions>
          <Button onClick={() => setDeleteDialogOpen(false)}>取消</Button>
          <Button onClick={handleDeleteConfirm} color="error" variant="contained">
            刪除
          </Button>
        </DialogActions>
      </Dialog>
    </Container>
  );
};

// 新增拜訪表單組件
const AddVisitForm: React.FC<{
  restaurant: any;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}> = ({ restaurant, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const { addVisitRecord } = useAppContext();
  const [visitDate, setVisitDate] = useState(new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]);
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<number>(5);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('');
  const [totalSpent, setTotalSpent] = useState(0);

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    try {
      const newVisit = {
        restaurantId: restaurant.id,
        visitDate: visitDate, // Just send the string, not Date object
        rating,
        comment: comment || undefined,
        dishes: [],
        totalSpent: totalSpent || 0,
        photos: [],
      };

      await addVisitRecord(newVisit as any);
      onSave();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to add visit:', error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Box sx={{ pt: 2 }}>
      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="拜訪日期"
        type="date"
        value={visitDate}
        onChange={(e) => setVisitDate(e.target.value)}
        InputLabelProps={{ shrink: true }}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />
      
      <Box sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
        <Typography variant="body2" gutterBottom>
          評分
        </Typography>
        <Rating
          value={rating}
          onChange={(_, newValue) => setRating(newValue || 5)}
        />
      </Box>

      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="總花費 (NT$)"
        type="number"
        value={totalSpent}
        onChange={(e) => setTotalSpent(Number(e.target.value))}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />

      <TextField
        fullWidth
        label="備註"
        multiline
        rows={3}
        value={comment}
        onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)}
        sx={{ mb: 2 }}
      />

      <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 1 }}>
        <Button onClick={onClose}>取消</Button>
        <Button variant="contained" onClick={handleSubmit}>
          儲存
        </Button>
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

export default RestaurantDetailPage;

```

檢查後端 controller 的回應格式：


後端回應只包含 `id`，需要回傳完整 visit 記錄。檢視資料庫架構：


問題在 `createVisit`：回應只含 `id`，前端需要完整紀錄。調整為回傳完整 visit 物件：


```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';

export const getVisits = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, startDate, endDate, start, end } = req.query;

    let query = `
      SELECT vr.*, r.name as restaurant_name, r.address as restaurant_address
      FROM visit_records vr
      JOIN restaurants r ON vr.restaurant_id = r.id
      WHERE vr.user_id = ?
    `;

    const params: any[] = [userId];

    if (restaurantId) {
      query += ` AND vr.restaurant_id = ?`;
      params.push(restaurantId);
    }

    if (startDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date >= ?`;
      params.push(startDate);
    }

    if (endDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date <= ?`;
      params.push(endDate);
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY vr.visit_date DESC`;

    // 分頁
    if (start && end) {
      query += ` LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`;
      params.push(parseInt(end as string) - parseInt(start as string), parseInt(start as string));
    }

    const visits = db.prepare(query).all(...params) as any[];

    // 取得餐點資料
    for (const visit of visits) {
      const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visit.id);
      visit.dishes = dishes;

      const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
        .all(visit.id)
        .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
      visit.photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: visits });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visits:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visits' });
  }
};

export const getVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId) as any;

    if (!visit) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 取得餐廳資訊
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, address FROM restaurants WHERE id = ?')
      .get((visit as any).restaurant_id);
    (visit as any).restaurant = restaurant;

    // 取得餐點
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(id);
    (visit as any).dishes = dishes;

    // 取得照片
    const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(id)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    (visit as any).photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;

    res.json({ success: true, data: visit });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visit' });
  }
};

export const createVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    if (!restaurantId || !visitDate || !rating) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant ID, visit date, and rating are required' });
    }

    // 驗證餐廳屬於該使用者
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(restaurantId, userId);

    if (!restaurant) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    const visitId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO visit_records (id, restaurant_id, user_id, visit_date, rating, comment, total_spent, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      visitId,
      restaurantId,
      userId,
      new Date(visitDate).toISOString(),
      rating,
      comment || null,
      totalSpent || 0,
      now,
      now
    );

    // 處理餐點
    if (dishes && Array.isArray(dishes)) {
      for (const dish of dishes) {
        const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(
          dishId,
          visitId,
          dish.name,
          dish.price || 0,
          dish.rating || null,
          dish.comment || null
        );
      }
    }

    // 處理照片
    if (photos && Array.isArray(photos)) {
      for (const photoUrl of photos) {
        const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(photoId, visitId, photoUrl);
      }
    }

    // 回傳完整的 visit 紀錄
    const newVisit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').get(visitId) as any;
    
    // 加上餐點和照片資料
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visitId);
    newVisit.dishes = dishes;

    const photoUrls = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(visitId)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    newVisit.photos = photoUrls.length > 0 ? photoUrls : undefined;

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: newVisit, message: 'Visit created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create visit' });
  }
};

export const updateVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    let updateQuery = 'UPDATE visit_records SET updated_at = ?';
    const updateParams: any[] = [new Date().toISOString()];

    if (visitDate) {
      updateQuery += ', visit_date = ?';
      updateParams.push(new Date(visitDate).toISOString());
    }
    if (rating !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', rating = ?';
      updateParams.push(rating);
    }
    if (comment !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', comment = ?';
      updateParams.push(comment || null);
    }
    if (totalSpent !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', total_spent = ?';
      updateParams.push(totalSpent);
    }

    updateQuery += ' WHERE id = ?';
    updateParams.push(id);

    db.prepare(updateQuery).run(...updateParams);

    // 更新餐點
    if (dishes !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊餐點
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新餐點
      if (Array.isArray(dishes)) {
        for (const dish of dishes) {
          const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            dishId,
            id,
            dish.name,
            dish.price || 0,
            dish.rating || null,
            dish.comment || null
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 更新照片
    if (photos !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊照片
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新照片
      if (Array.isArray(photos)) {
        for (const photoUrl of photos) {
          const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
            .run(photoId, id, photoUrl);
        }
      }
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit updated successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to update visit' });
  }
};

export const deleteVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete visit' });
  }
};

```

檢查 createVisit 中是否有 `crypto` 導入：


需要導入 `crypto` 模組：


```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';
import crypto from 'crypto';

export const getVisits = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, startDate, endDate, start, end } = req.query;

    let query = `
      SELECT vr.*, r.name as restaurant_name, r.address as restaurant_address
      FROM visit_records vr
      JOIN restaurants r ON vr.restaurant_id = r.id
      WHERE vr.user_id = ?
    `;

    const params: any[] = [userId];

    if (restaurantId) {
      query += ` AND vr.restaurant_id = ?`;
      params.push(restaurantId);
    }

    if (startDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date >= ?`;
      params.push(startDate);
    }

    if (endDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date <= ?`;
      params.push(endDate);
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY vr.visit_date DESC`;

    // 分頁
    if (start && end) {
      query += ` LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`;
      params.push(parseInt(end as string) - parseInt(start as string), parseInt(start as string));
    }

    const visits = db.prepare(query).all(...params) as any[];

    // 取得餐點資料
    for (const visit of visits) {
      const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visit.id);
      visit.dishes = dishes;

      const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
        .all(visit.id)
        .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
      visit.photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: visits });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visits:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visits' });
  }
};

export const getVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId) as any;

    if (!visit) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 取得餐廳資訊
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, address FROM restaurants WHERE id = ?')
      .get((visit as any).restaurant_id);
    (visit as any).restaurant = restaurant;

    // 取得餐點
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(id);
    (visit as any).dishes = dishes;

    // 取得照片
    const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(id)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    (visit as any).photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;

    res.json({ success: true, data: visit });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visit' });
  }
};

export const createVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    if (!restaurantId || !visitDate || !rating) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant ID, visit date, and rating are required' });
    }

    // 驗證餐廳屬於該使用者
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(restaurantId, userId);

    if (!restaurant) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    const visitId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO visit_records (id, restaurant_id, user_id, visit_date, rating, comment, total_spent, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      visitId,
      restaurantId,
      userId,
      new Date(visitDate).toISOString(),
      rating,
      comment || null,
      totalSpent || 0,
      now,
      now
    );

    // 處理餐點
    if (dishes && Array.isArray(dishes)) {
      for (const dish of dishes) {
        const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(
          dishId,
          visitId,
          dish.name,
          dish.price || 0,
          dish.rating || null,
          dish.comment || null
        );
      }
    }

    // 處理照片
    if (photos && Array.isArray(photos)) {
      for (const photoUrl of photos) {
        const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(photoId, visitId, photoUrl);
      }
    }

    // 回傳完整的 visit 紀錄
    const newVisit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').get(visitId) as any;
    
    // 加上餐點和照片資料
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visitId);
    newVisit.dishes = dishes;

    const photoUrls = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(visitId)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    newVisit.photos = photoUrls.length > 0 ? photoUrls : undefined;

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: newVisit, message: 'Visit created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create visit' });
  }
};

export const updateVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    let updateQuery = 'UPDATE visit_records SET updated_at = ?';
    const updateParams: any[] = [new Date().toISOString()];

    if (visitDate) {
      updateQuery += ', visit_date = ?';
      updateParams.push(new Date(visitDate).toISOString());
    }
    if (rating !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', rating = ?';
      updateParams.push(rating);
    }
    if (comment !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', comment = ?';
      updateParams.push(comment || null);
    }
    if (totalSpent !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', total_spent = ?';
      updateParams.push(totalSpent);
    }

    updateQuery += ' WHERE id = ?';
    updateParams.push(id);

    db.prepare(updateQuery).run(...updateParams);

    // 更新餐點
    if (dishes !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊餐點
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新餐點
      if (Array.isArray(dishes)) {
        for (const dish of dishes) {
          const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            dishId,
            id,
            dish.name,
            dish.price || 0,
            dish.rating || null,
            dish.comment || null
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 更新照片
    if (photos !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊照片
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新照片
      if (Array.isArray(photos)) {
        for (const photoUrl of photos) {
          const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
            .run(photoId, id, photoUrl);
        }
      }
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit updated successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to update visit' });
  }
};

export const deleteVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete visit' });
  }
};

```

檢查是否需要修正欄位名稱（snake_case vs camelCase）：


檢查資料庫欄位名稱：


資料庫欄位是 snake_case（`visit_date`, `total_spent`），但回傳的資料可能仍為 snake_case。檢查如何轉換為 camelCase：


檢查其他 controller 如何回傳資料：


欄位直接使用 snake_case。檢查前端型別定義，確認欄位名稱：


檢查型別定義：


前端使用 camelCase，後端回傳 snake_case。於後端 controller 統一轉換為 camelCase。


```typescript
import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.middleware';
import db from '../utils/db';
import crypto from 'crypto';

export const getVisits = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, startDate, endDate, start, end } = req.query;

    let query = `
      SELECT vr.*, r.name as restaurant_name, r.address as restaurant_address
      FROM visit_records vr
      JOIN restaurants r ON vr.restaurant_id = r.id
      WHERE vr.user_id = ?
    `;

    const params: any[] = [userId];

    if (restaurantId) {
      query += ` AND vr.restaurant_id = ?`;
      params.push(restaurantId);
    }

    if (startDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date >= ?`;
      params.push(startDate);
    }

    if (endDate) {
      query += ` AND vr.visit_date <= ?`;
      params.push(endDate);
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY vr.visit_date DESC`;

    // 分頁
    if (start && end) {
      query += ` LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`;
      params.push(parseInt(end as string) - parseInt(start as string), parseInt(start as string));
    }

    const visits = db.prepare(query).all(...params) as any[];

    // 取得餐點資料
    for (const visit of visits) {
      const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visit.id);
      visit.dishes = dishes;

      const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
        .all(visit.id)
        .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
      visit.photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;
    }

    res.json({ success: true, data: visits });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visits:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visits' });
  }
};

export const getVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId) as any;

    if (!visit) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 取得餐廳資訊
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, address FROM restaurants WHERE id = ?')
      .get((visit as any).restaurant_id);
    (visit as any).restaurant = restaurant;

    // 取得餐點
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(id);
    (visit as any).dishes = dishes;

    // 取得照片
    const photos = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(id)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);
    (visit as any).photos = photos.length > 0 ? photos : undefined;

    res.json({ success: true, data: visit });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error getting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to get visit' });
  }
};

export const createVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { restaurantId, visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    if (!restaurantId || !visitDate || !rating) {
      return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant ID, visit date, and rating are required' });
    }

    // 驗證餐廳屬於該使用者
    const restaurant = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(restaurantId, userId);

    if (!restaurant) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Restaurant not found' });
    }

    const visitId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();

    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO visit_records (id, restaurant_id, user_id, visit_date, rating, comment, total_spent, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      visitId,
      restaurantId,
      userId,
      new Date(visitDate).toISOString(),
      rating,
      comment || null,
      totalSpent || 0,
      now,
      now
    );

    // 處理餐點
    if (dishes && Array.isArray(dishes)) {
      for (const dish of dishes) {
        const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare(`
          INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `).run(
          dishId,
          visitId,
          dish.name,
          dish.price || 0,
          dish.rating || null,
          dish.comment || null
        );
      }
    }

    // 處理照片
    if (photos && Array.isArray(photos)) {
      for (const photoUrl of photos) {
        const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
          .run(photoId, visitId, photoUrl);
      }
    }

    // 回傳完整的 visit 紀錄
    const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').get(visitId) as any;
    
    // 加上餐點和照片資料
    const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visitId);
    const photoUrls = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
      .all(visitId)
      .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);

    // 轉換為 camelCase
    const formattedVisit = {
      id: visit.id,
      restaurantId: visit.restaurant_id,
      userId: visit.user_id,
      visitDate: new Date(visit.visit_date),
      rating: visit.rating,
      comment: visit.comment,
      dishes: dishes,
      totalSpent: visit.total_spent,
      photos: photoUrls.length > 0 ? photoUrls : undefined,
      createdAt: new Date(visit.created_at),
      updatedAt: new Date(visit.updated_at)
    };

    res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: formattedVisit, message: 'Visit created successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error creating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to create visit' });
  }
};

export const updateVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;
    const { visitDate, rating, comment, dishes, totalSpent, photos } = req.body;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    let updateQuery = 'UPDATE visit_records SET updated_at = ?';
    const updateParams: any[] = [new Date().toISOString()];

    if (visitDate) {
      updateQuery += ', visit_date = ?';
      updateParams.push(new Date(visitDate).toISOString());
    }
    if (rating !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', rating = ?';
      updateParams.push(rating);
    }
    if (comment !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', comment = ?';
      updateParams.push(comment || null);
    }
    if (totalSpent !== undefined) {
      updateQuery += ', total_spent = ?';
      updateParams.push(totalSpent);
    }

    updateQuery += ' WHERE id = ?';
    updateParams.push(id);

    db.prepare(updateQuery).run(...updateParams);

    // 更新餐點
    if (dishes !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊餐點
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新餐點
      if (Array.isArray(dishes)) {
        for (const dish of dishes) {
          const dishId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO dishes (id, visit_id, name, price, rating, comment)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            dishId,
            id,
            dish.name,
            dish.price || 0,
            dish.rating || null,
            dish.comment || null
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // 更新照片
    if (photos !== undefined) {
      // 刪除舊照片
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?').run(id);
      // 新增新照片
      if (Array.isArray(photos)) {
        for (const photoUrl of photos) {
          const photoId = crypto.randomUUID();
          db.prepare('INSERT INTO visit_photos (id, visit_id, photo_url) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
            .run(photoId, id, photoUrl);
        }
      }
    }

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit updated successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error updating visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to update visit' });
  }
};

export const deleteVisit = async (req: AuthRequest, res: Response) => {
  try {
    const { id } = req.params;
    const userId = req.user!.userId;

    // 檢查拜訪紀錄是否存在
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM visit_records WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?')
      .get(id, userId);

    if (!existing) {
      return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'Visit not found' });
    }

    // 由於有外鍵約束，相關資料會自動刪除
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').run(id);

    res.json({ success: true, message: 'Visit deleted successfully' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error deleting visit:', error);
    res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: 'Failed to delete visit' });
  }
};

```

檢查是否有 Lint 錯誤：


摘要修正內容：

## ✅ 已完成的修復

### 問題診斷
無法新增拜訪紀錄的原因：
1. 前端傳送不必要欄位（`id`, `userId`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` 等）
2. 後端僅回傳 `{ id: visitId }`，而非完整 visit
3. 格式：資料庫欄位為 snake_case，需轉為前端的 camelCase

### 修復內容

#### 1. 前端修復（`RestaurantDetailPage.tsx`）
- 修正 `handleSubmit`，僅送出必要欄位
- 將 `Date` 改為字串（`visitDate: visitDate`）
- 將呼叫改為 async，補上錯誤處理

**修正後的程式碼：**
```typescript
const handleSubmit = async () => {
  try {
    const newVisit = {
      restaurantId: restaurant.id,
      visitDate: visitDate, // Just send the string, not Date object
      rating,
      comment: comment || undefined,
      dishes: [],
      totalSpent: totalSpent || 0,
      photos: [],
    };

    await addVisitRecord(newVisit as any);
    onSave();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to add visit:', error);
  }
};
```

#### 2. 後端修復（`visit.controller.ts`）
- 調整 `createVisit` 為回傳完整 visit 紀錄
- 加入 snake_case → camelCase 轉換
- 補充導入 `crypto`

**修正後的程式碼：**
```typescript
// 回傳完整的 visit 紀錄
const visit = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM visit_records WHERE id = ?').get(visitId) as any;

// 加上餐點和照片資料
const dishes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM dishes WHERE visit_id = ?').all(visitId);
const photoUrls = db.prepare('SELECT photo_url FROM visit_photos WHERE visit_id = ?')
  .all(visitId)
  .map((row: any) => row.photo_url);

// 轉換為 camelCase
const formattedVisit = {
  id: visit.id,
  restaurantId: visit.restaurant_id,
  userId: visit.user_id,
  visitDate: new Date(visit.visit_date),
  rating: visit.rating,
  comment: visit.comment,
  dishes: dishes,
  totalSpent: visit.total_spent,
  photos: photoUrls.length > 0 ? photoUrls : undefined,
  createdAt: new Date(visit.created_at),
  updatedAt: new Date(visit.updated_at)
};

res.status(201).json({ success: true, data: formattedVisit, message: 'Visit created successfully' });
```

### 測試步驟
1. 進入餐廳詳情頁
2. 點「新增拜訪」
3. 填寫日期、評分、花費、備註
4. 點「儲存」
5. 應可正常新增並顯示

此為 Google Maps API 的棄用提示，不影響功能，可暫時忽略或後續升級至 `AdvancedMarkerElement`。
